var TCVSIDE=(function(){
 const S=(typeof TCVREC_SIDE!=='undefined'&&TCVREC_SIDE)||{lines:{},cast:{},names:{},by:{},mis:{},ids:{}},R=(typeof TCVREC!=='undefined'&&TCVREC)||null;
 const st={recs:0,merged:0,cast:0,castKept:[],calls:0,traits:0,missions:0,thanks:0,phone:0,skipped:0,stopped:0,last:null,err:null};
 const err=(w,e)=>{st.err=w+': '+String(e&&e.message||e).slice(0,120)};
  
 try{if(R){const D=R.D;D.lines=D.lines||{};
   for(const t in S.lines){const L=D.lines[t]||(D.lines[t]={});for(const v in S.lines[t]){st.recs++;if(!L[v]){L[v]=S.lines[t][v];R.st.n++;st.merged++}}}
   R.st.bytes=(R.st.bytes||0)+(S.bytes||0);
   if(D.modes){D.names=D.names||{};for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(S.cast||{})){for(const k in D.modes){const M=D.modes[k];M.cast=M.cast||{};if(!M.cast[id]){M.cast[id]=v;st.cast++}else if(M.cast[id]!==v&&!st.castKept.includes(id))st.castKept.push(id)}if(!D.names[id])D.names[id]=(S.names||{})[id]||id}}}
  else st.err='אין TCVREC'}catch(e){err('merge',e)}
 const T=id=>(S.ids||{})[id]||'';
 const voiceOf=(who,text)=>{try{return R&&R.voiceFor(who,text)||''}catch(e){return ''}};
 const hasRec=(who,id)=>{const t=T(id),v=voiceOf(who,t);return !!(t&&v&&R.rec(t,v))};
 const off=()=>{try{if(!R||R.mode==='off')return true;if(typeof TCSET!=='undefined'&&TCSET.voiceOff())return true;return !(PlayerProfile.settings.audio)}catch(e){return true}};
 const busy=()=>{try{if(typeof TCSHOW!=='undefined'&&TCSHOW.on)return true;if(typeof TCCUT!=='undefined'&&TCCUT.playing&&TCCUT.playing())return true}catch(e){}return false};
 const mem=()=>{const s=g77St();return s.vsd||(s.vsd={n:{},t:{}})};
 const pick=(who,kind)=>{const L=((S.by||{})[who]||{})[kind]||[],ok=L.filter(id=>hasRec(who,id));if(!ok.length)return '';const M=mem(),k=kind+':'+who,i=(M.n[k]||0)%ok.length;M.n[k]=(M.n[k]||0)+1;return ok[i]};
  
 let cap=null,mine=0;
 function capEl(){if(cap)return cap;cap=document.createElement('div');cap.id='tcVsdCap';cap.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:118px;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:57;max-width:min(620px,90vw);direction:rtl;font:15px/1.45 "Segoe UI",Arial,sans-serif;color:#f3f7fa;background:rgba(12,22,32,.9);border:1px solid #3a6a8a;border-radius:12px;padding:7px 14px;display:none;pointer-events:none';document.body.appendChild(cap);return cap}
 function caption(who,text,inCall){try{const nm=(g77S().chars[who]||{}).name||(S.names||{})[who]||'';
   if(inCall){const d=g77S().el;if(d){let c=d.querySelector('[data-vsd]');if(!c){c=document.createElement('div');c.setAttribute('data-vsd','1');c.style.cssText='margin:2px 0 6px;color:#cfe6f5;font-style:italic';const tx=d.querySelector('.tx');d.insertBefore(c,tx)}c.textContent='"'+text+'"';return}}
   const e=capEl();e.textContent=(nm?nm+': ':'')+text;e.style.display='block'}catch(e){err('cap',e)}}
 function uncap(){try{if(cap)cap.style.display='none';const d=g77S().el,c=d&&d.querySelector('[data-vsd]');if(c)c.remove()}catch(e){}}
  
 function say(who,id,kind,o){o=o||{};const t=T(id);if(!t||!hasRec(who,id)){st.skipped++;return false}if(off()||busy()){st.skipped++;return false}
  const my=++mine;let ok=false;
  try{ok=TCVOICE.speak(t,who,'side',1,{onstart(){if(my===mine||o.chain)caption(who,t,o.inCall)},onend(){if(my===mine){uncap();if(o.after)o.after()}}})}catch(e){err('say',e);ok=false}
  if(ok){st[kind]++;st.last={who,id,kind}}return ok}
  
 function note(who,id,heard){try{const m=g77MsgIn(who,T(id),{voice:true,silent:true});if(m&&heard)m.read=true;try{g77SpBadge()}catch(e){}return m}catch(e){err('note',e);return null}}
  
 const _in=g77MsgIn;
 g77MsgIn=function(who,text,o){let v='';try{if(o&&o.voice&&R&&R.mode!=='off'&&(S.cast||{})[who]){const t=g77MsgClean(text);v=voiceOf(who,t);const r=v&&R.rec(t,v);if(r)o=Object.assign({},o,{dur:Math.max(1,Math.round(r[0]))});else v=''}}catch(e){v=''}
  const m=_in.apply(this,[who,text,o]);try{if(m&&v&&m.vv!==v){m.vv=v;st.phone++}}catch(e){}return m};
  
 const _ans=g77SpAnswer;
 g77SpAnswer=function(){let rec=null;try{const P=g77Sp();rec=P.ring&&P.ring.rec}catch(e){}const r=_ans.apply(this,arguments);
  try{if(r&&rec&&rec.who&&(S.by||{})[rec.who]){const who=rec.who,id=pick(who,'call');if(id){const M=mem(),k='c:'+who;M.t[k]=(M.t[k]||0)+1;const tr=M.t[k]%2===0?pick(who,'trait'):'';
     say(who,id,'calls',{inCall:true,after:tr?()=>{if(g77S().open)say(who,tr,'traits',{inCall:true})}:null})}}}catch(e){err('call',e)}return r};
 const _close=g77DlgClose;

 function onRoad(x,y){roads();for(const r of RD){const px=x-r.ax,py=y-r.ay,t=px*r.ux+py*r.uy,o=Math.abs(-px*r.uy+py*r.ux);if(t>=-r.h&&t<=r.L+r.h&&o<r.h+3){for(const c of XW){const qx=x-c.x,qy=y-c.y;if(Math.abs(qx*c.ux+qy*c.uy)<=c.dep&&Math.abs(-qx*c.uy+qy*c.ux)<=c.h+2)return null}return r}}return null}
 function walkOk(x,y){if(onRoad(x,y))return false;try{if(typeof staticPersonBlockedAt==='function'&&staticPersonBlockedAt(x,y,3))return false}catch(e){}return true}
 

 function spotNear(p,avoid){let best=null,bs=1e9;
  for(let r=0;r<=150;r+=10)for(let k=0;k<(r?24:1);k++){const a=k/24*TAU,x=p.x+Math.cos(a)*r,y=p.y+Math.sin(a)*r;if(avoid&&Math.hypot(x-avoid.x,y-avoid.y)<38)continue;const sc=r+(Math.sin(a)<-.2?9:0);if(sc>=bs)continue;if(!walkOk(x,y))continue;bs=sc;best={x,y}}
  if(!best)return null;let from={...best};const ox=avoid?avoid.x:p.x,oy=avoid?avoid.y:p.y-1,ax=best.x-ox,ay=best.y-oy,L=Math.hypot(ax,ay)||1;
  for(let s=8;s<=48;s+=8){const x=best.x+ax/L*s,y=best.y+ay/L*s;if(!walkOk(x,y))break;from={x,y}}
  return{from,to:best}}

 function snap(){const m=mstate();if(!m)return null;return{m,ch:m.ch,mi:m.mi,step:m.step,done:Object.keys(m.done||{}),t0:m.t0||0,end:!!m.end}}
 function chOf(mi){for(const c of SD().chapters||[])for(const x of c.missions||[])if(x.id===mi)return c.id;return null}
  
 function events(a,b,now){const out=[];if(!b)return out;
  if(!a||a.m!==b.m){if(!b.done.length&&b.mi&&b.step===0&&Math.abs((now==null?gm():now)-b.t0)<=3)out.push('start:'+b.mi);return out}
  const nd=b.done.filter(x=>!a.done.includes(x));
  if(nd.length>1){if(b.mi&&b.mi!==a.mi&&b.step===0)out.push('start:'+b.mi);return out}    
  for(const x of nd)out.push('done:'+x);
  if(a.ch!==b.ch&&a.ch!=null){out.push('chapter:'+a.ch)}
  if(b.mi&&b.mi!==a.mi){out.push('start:'+b.mi);for(let i=1;i<=b.step;i++)out.push('step:'+b.mi+'/'+i)}
  else if(b.mi&&b.mi===a.mi&&b.step>a.step)for(let i=a.step+1;i<=b.step;i++)out.push('step:'+b.mi+'/'+i);
  if(b.end&&!a.end&&a.ch!=null&&a.ch===b.ch)out.push('chapter:'+b.ch);
  return out}
 function scenesFor(ev){const L=(D.on[ev]||[]).slice();if(D.chapterEnd&&/^chapter:/.test(ev)){const c=ev.slice(8);if(c!=='O')L.unshift('end:'+c)}return L}
 function enqueue(id,o){o=o||{};if(!scene(id))return false;if(!o.force&&ST.q.some(q=>q.id===id))return false;if(ST.on&&ST.on.id===id&&!o.force)return false;
  ST.q.push({id,at:performance.now(),force:!!o.force,why:o.why||''});return true}
 function poll(){ST.poll++;let E=null;try{E=TCSTORY.E}catch(e){}if(E&&E.qa)return;const s=snap();
  if(s){let evs=[];try{evs=events(ST.base,s)}catch(e){err('events',e)}ST.base=s?{...s,done:s.done.slice()}:null;
   if(evs.length&&autoOk())for(const ev of evs)for(const id of scenesFor(ev)){if(seen(id))continue;mark(id);if(enqueue(id,{why:ev}))log('queue',id,ev)}}
  else ST.base=null;
  if(!ST.on)tryStart()}
 function log(k,id,x){if(ST.log.length>120)ST.log.splice(0,40);ST.log.push([k,id,x||'',Math.round(performance.now())])}
  
 function scene(id){if(D.scenes[id])return D.scenes[id];const m=/^end:(.+)$/.exec(id);if(!m)return null;const c=(SD().chapters||[]).find(q=>q.id===m[1]);if(!c)return null;
  const row=c.row>=0?(SD().list||[])[c.row]:'';return{ch:c.id,name:c.no+' הושלם',gen:true,shots:[{d:5,cam:{follow:'player',z:.62},title:[c.no+' הושלם',c.name],card:row?{n:c.row+1,t:row}:null},{d:1.4,cam:{follow:'player',z:'back'}}]}}
 function list(){const L=[];for(const c of SD().chapters||[]){if(c.id!=='O')L.push('end:'+c.id);for(const [id,s] of Object.entries(D.scenes))if(s.ch===c.id)L.push(id)}
  const ord=x=>{const s=scene(x);const i=(SD().chapters||[]).findIndex(c=>c.id===(s&&s.ch));return i*10+(/^end:/.test(x)?9:0)};return [...new Set(L)].sort((a,b)=>ord(a)-ord(b))}
  
 function s15tc(){try{return s15Tc(s15Clock().abs)}catch(e){return null}}
 function landInfo(){const tc=s15tc();if(tc==null||typeof S15SIM==='undefined')return null;const T=S15SIM.T;let best=null;
  for(const f of S15SIM.flights){for(const g of f.legs){if(!(g.z0>0&&g.z1===0))continue;const x0=g.p[0][0],x1=g.p[g.p.length-1][0],v=(x1-x0)/Math.max(.1,g.t1-g.t0),tIn=g.t0+(3500-x0)/Math.max(1,v);    
    let dt=((tIn-tc)%T+T)%T;if(dt>T-3)dt-=T;if(!best||dt<best.dt)best={f,g,dt,tIn}}}return best}
 function waitOk(sc){if(sc.await!=='land')return true;const L=landInfo();if(!L)return true;const lead=sc.shots[0].d;return L.dt>=1.8&&L.dt<=lead+.6}
 function tryStart(){if(!ST.q.length)return;const now=performance.now();ST.q=ST.q.filter(q=>{const sc=scene(q.id);const life=sc&&sc.await?12*60e3:4*60e3;return q.force||now-q.at<life});if(!ST.q.length)return;
  const b=whyBusy();if(b&&!(ST.q[0].force&&(b==='driving'||b==='mission')))return;
  for(let i=0;i<ST.q.length;i++){const q=ST.q[i],sc=scene(q.id);if(!sc){ST.q.splice(i,1);i--;continue}if(!waitOk(sc))continue;if(sc.need==='bus3'&&!bus3()){if(!q.force&&now-q.at>30e3){ST.q.splice(i,1);i--}continue}
   ST.q.splice(i,1);start(q.id,sc,q);return}}
  
 function bus3(near){try{let best=null,d0=1e18;const c=near||playerXY();for(const b of LN.bus){if(String(b.L)!=='3'||!b.v||b.ph==='park')continue;const d=Math.hypot(b.v.x-c.x,b.v.y-c.y);if(d<d0){d0=d;best=b}}return best}catch(e){return null}}
 function playerXY(){try{const a=playerActor();return{x:a.x,y:a.y}}catch(e){return{x:Camera.x,y:Camera.y}}}
 function landPose(S){const f=S.land&&S.land.f;if(!f)return null;const tc=s15tc();if(tc==null)return null;const P=s15PoseAt(f,tc);if(!P)return null;const k=(typeof S15_K!=='undefined'?S15_K:.6);return{x:P[0],y:P[1]-k*(P[3]||0),z:P[3]||0,raw:P}}
 function tugPose(){try{const S=window.__TC_S14;if(!S)return null;const t=S.dayT(S.gmin())[1],T1=S.D.tug1||S.D.tug||null;let P=null;
   for(const id of ['TUG-1','tug','TUG1']){try{P=S.poseOf(id,t)}catch(e){P=null}if(P&&P.attached)return{x:P.x,y:P.y,att:true}}
   const r=S.D.tug_pier&&S.D.tug_pier.rect;if(r)return{x:(r[0]+r[2])/2+60,y:(r[1]+r[3])/2,att:false}}catch(e){}return null}
 function target(S,c){const f=c.follow;let p=null;
  if(f==='player')p=playerXY();else if(f==='car')p={x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y};else if(f&&f.startsWith('actor:')){const A=S.actors[f.slice(6)];p=A?{x:A.x,y:A.y-14}:null}
  else if(f==='bus3'){if(!S.bus||!S.bus.v||S.bus.ph==='park')S.bus=bus3(S.bus&&S.bus.v?S.bus.v:null);p=S.bus&&S.bus.v?{x:S.bus.v.x,y:S.bus.v.y}:null}
  else if(f==='land'){p=landPose(S)}else if(f==='tug'){p=tugPose()}
  else if(c.poi){const q=poiXY(c.poi);p=q?{x:q.x+(c.dx||0),y:q.y+(c.dy||0)}:null}
  return p}

 function voice(s){try{if(ST.qaRun||!s||!s[0])return;const t=lineText(s);if(!t||typeof TCVREC==='undefined'||typeof TCVOICE==='undefined')return;const v=TCVREC.voiceFor(s[0],t)||(typeof TCVREC_ST!=='undefined'?TCVREC_ST.voiceOf(t,s[0]):'');

 function engineSound(){try{if(ST.qaRun)return;if(typeof TCAUDIO==='undefined'||!TCAUDIO.ctx)return;const c=TCAUDIO.ctx();if(!c||c.state!=='running')return;const out=(TCAUDIO.bus&&TCAUDIO.bus.sfx)||c.destination,t=c.currentTime+.05;
   const g=c.createGain();g.gain.value=0;g.connect(out);const o=c.createOscillator();o.type='sawtooth';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(70,t);o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(95,t+.8);
   const lp=c.createBiquadFilter();lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.value=480;o.connect(lp);lp.connect(g);const tr=c.createOscillator(),tg=c.createGain();tr.frequency.value=11;tg.gain.value=.05;tr.connect(tg);tg.connect(g.gain);
   g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(.09,t+.06);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.09,t+.85);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(.22,t+.95);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.06,t+1.6);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.06,t+2.6);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0,t+3);
   o.frequency.setValueAtTime(48,t+.9);o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(36,t+1.6);o.start(t);tr.start(t);o.stop(t+3.05);tr.stop(t+3.05);ST.on.sound=1}catch(e){err('sound',e)}}
  
 function makeActor(S,a){const lk=look(a.who);if(!lk)return null;let p=null,toward=playerXY();
  if(a.near==='car'){const c={x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y};p=spotNear(c,c);toward=c}
  else{const q=poiXY(a.at);if(!q)return null;p=spotNear(q,toward)}if(!p)return null;
  const from=a.walk?p.from:p.to,to=p.to;return{id:a.id,who:a.who,look:lk,x:from.x,y:from.y,fx:from.x,fy:from.y,tx:to.x,ty:to.y,t0:S.t,walk:Math.hypot(to.x-from.x,to.y-from.y)>2?Math.max(1.4,Math.hypot(to.x-from.x,to.y-from.y)/26):0,h:Math.PI/2,ph:0,act:'stand',face:toward,at:a.at||null,dist:0}}
 function actorStep(A,t){if(A.walk&&t-A.t0<A.walk){const u=sm((t-A.t0)/A.walk),x=A.fx+(A.tx-A.fx)*u,y=A.fy+(A.ty-A.fy)*u;A.dist+=Math.hypot(x-A.x,y-A.y);A.h=Math.atan2(A.ty-A.fy,A.tx-A.fx);A.x=x;A.y=y;A.act='walk'}
  else{A.x=A.tx;A.y=A.ty;if(A.act==='walk')A.act='stand';if(A.face){const dx=A.face.x-A.x,dy=A.face.y-A.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>4)A.h=Math.atan2(dy,dx)}}}
 function drawMan(A,now){if(!window.P34){try{if(typeof p34LibInit==='function')p34LibInit()}catch(e){}if(!window.P34)return}if(!e3InView(A.x-20,A.y-50,40,60,30))return;
  const res=(typeof p599Res==='function')?p599Res(Camera.zoom*(window.devicePixelRatio||1)):1,k=A.act==='wave'?'marshal':A.act==='walk'?'walk':'stand',ph=A.act==='walk'?(A.dist/22)%1:A.act==='wave'?((now/1000)*.9)%1:((now/1000)*.15)%1,g=ctx;
  const f=()=>{try{P34.draw(g,A.x,A.y,A.look,{k},A.h,ph,res)}catch(e){try{P34.draw(g,A.x,A.y,A.look,{k:'stand'},A.h,0,res)}catch(e2){err('p34',e2)}}};
  try{if(typeof C34G!=='undefined'&&C34G.on&&C34G.armed&&C34G.q&&typeof c34Live==='function'&&c34Live())C34G.q.push({y:A.y,f});else f()}catch(e){f()}}
 function drawPuffs(S,now){const P=S.puffs;if(!P||!P.length)return;const g=ctx;g.save();for(const q of P){const u=(now-q.t)/q.life;if(u<0||u>1)continue;g.globalAlpha=(1-u)*.55;g.fillStyle=q.c;g.beginPath();g.arc(q.x+q.vx*u,q.y+q.vy*u-u*10,q.r*(.5+u*1.6),0,TAU);g.fill()}g.restore();S.puffs=P.filter(q=>now-q.t<q.life)}
 function puff(S,now,n){const h=PlayerCar.heading||0,L=20;S.puffs=S.puffs||[];for(let i=0;i<(n||1);i++)S.puffs.push({x:PlayerCar.x-Math.cos(h)*L+Math.sin(h)*5,y:PlayerCar.y-Math.sin(h)*L-Math.cos(h)*5,vx:-Math.cos(h)*14+(Math.random()-.5)*8,vy:-Math.sin(h)*14-4,r:3+Math.random()*2.5,life:900+Math.random()*700,t:now+i*60,c:i%2?'#dfe5ea':'#bfc7ce'})}
  
 function presence(now){if(ST.on)return;const m=mstate();let C=null;try{C=TCSTORY.cur()}catch(e){}const s=C&&C.s;if(!m||!s||!s.talk||!s.at){ST.presence.length=0;return}
  let who='';try{who=(SD().dialogs[s.talk]||{}).who||''}catch(e){}if(!D.looks[who]){ST.presence.length=0;return}

 function start(id,sc,q){const C=Camera;const S={id,sc,i:-1,t:0,ts:0,t0:performance.now(),last:performance.now(),save:{x:C.x,y:C.y,z:C.zoom},cam:{x:C.x,y:C.y,z:C.zoom},from:null,actors:{},bus:null,land:null,force:!!(q&&q.force),why:q&&q.why||'',puffs:[],shake:0};
  if(sc.await==='land'){const L=landInfo();if(L){S.land=L;if(L.dt>0&&L.dt<sc.shots[0].d+1)S.firstD=cl(L.dt-.2,1.6,sc.shots[0].d)}}    
  try{S.tcoUi0=typeof TCO!=='undefined'&&!!TCO.ui}catch(e){}ST.on=S;ST.played++;ST.last={id,at:Date.now()};log('start',id,S.why);
  try{Input.held.clear();Input.pressed.clear();Input.wheel=0}catch(e){}try{g77BbClear()}catch(e){}
  const d=ui();d.classList.remove('in');d.style.display='block';document.body.classList.add('tcCutOn');requestAnimationFrame(()=>d.classList.add('in'));
  S.max=Math.min(60,sc.shots.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.d||3),0)+6);next(S);if(!ST.raf)ST.raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);if(!ST.qaRun)try{SaveManager.log('story','cutscene',{id,state:'start'})}catch(e){}return S}
 function next(S){S.i++;const sh=S.sc.shots[S.i];if(!sh){end(false);return}S.ts=0;S.from={...S.cam};S.cut=false;
  if(sh.cam&&sh.cam.from){const p=target(S,{poi:sh.cam.from.poi,dx:sh.cam.from.dx,dy:sh.cam.from.dy});if(p){S.cam={x:p.x,y:p.y,z:sh.cam.from.z||S.cam.z};S.from={...S.cam};S.cut=true}}
  else if(sh.cam&&(sh.cam.poi||sh.cam.follow==='land'||sh.cam.follow==='tug'||sh.cam.follow==='bus3')&&S.i>0){const p=target(S,sh.cam);if(p&&Math.hypot(p.x-S.cam.x,p.y-S.cam.y)>2200/Math.max(.5,S.cam.z))S.cut=true}    
  else if(sh.cam&&S.i===0){const p=target(S,sh.cam);if(p&&Math.hypot(p.x-S.cam.x,p.y-S.cam.y)>2600)S.cut=true}
  if(S.cut){fade(true);S.cutT=.32}
  if(sh.title!==undefined)setTitle(sh.title);if(sh.say!==undefined){setSub(sh.say);voice(sh.say)}else if(S.i>0&&!S.sc.shots[S.i-1].say)setSub(null);
  setCard(sh.card||null);
  for(const a of sh.actors||[]){const A=makeActor(S,a);if(A)S.actors[a.id]=A}
  if(sh.act)for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(sh.act)){const A=S.actors[k];if(A){A.act=v;A.walk=0}}
  if(sh.bubble){const A=S.actors[sh.bubble[0]];const anc=A?()=>({x:A.x,y:A.y-30}):'player';try{g77Bubble(anc,sh.bubble[1],{who:A?A.who:'',ms:Math.max(2200,(sh.d||3)*1000-300)})}catch(e){err('bubble',e)}}
  if(sh.fx==='engine'){S.engine={t:S.t};engineSound()}}
 function camGoal(S,sh,dt){const c=sh.cam||{};let p=target(S,c);if(!p)p=S.lastP||playerXY();S.lastP=p;let z=c.z==='back'?S.save.z:(c.z||S.cam.z);
  if(c.follow&&c.follow!=='player'&&c.follow!=='car'&&!(c.follow.startsWith('actor:')))return{x:p.x,y:p.y,z,k:6};    
  return{x:p.x,y:p.y,z,k:c.follow?3.2:1.15}}
 function frame(){ST.raf=0;const S=ST.on;if(!S)return;const now=performance.now(),dt=Math.min(.1,(now-S.last)/1000);S.last=now;
  try{S.t+=dt;S.ts+=dt;const sh=S.sc.shots[S.i];if(!sh){end(false);return}
   if(S.cutT!=null){S.cutT-=dt;if(S.cutT<=0){S.cutT=null;if(!(sh.cam&&sh.cam.from)){const g=camGoal(S,sh,dt);S.cam={x:g.x,y:g.y,z:g.z}}const C=Camera;C.x=S.cam.x;C.y=S.cam.y;C.zoom=S.cam.z;fade(false)}}    
   for(const A of Object.values(S.actors))actorStep(A,S.t);
   if(S.engine){const u=S.t-S.engine.t;if(u<2.6&&Math.floor(u*8)!==S.engine.k){S.engine.k=Math.floor(u*8);puff(S,now,u<.9?1:u<1.3?3:1)}S.shake=u>.85&&u<1.5?(1.5-u)*2.4:u<.85?.5:0}else S.shake=0;
   const until=sh.until==='landed'?landed(S):false,len=S.i===0&&S.firstD?S.firstD:(sh.d||3);
   const p=ui().querySelector('.prog');if(p){const tot=S.sc.shots.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.d||3),0);let el=0;for(let i=0;i<S.i;i++)el+=S.sc.shots[i].d||3;p.style.width=cl((el+Math.min(S.ts,len))/tot*100,0,100).toFixed(1)+'%'}
   if(S.ts>=len||until||S.t>S.max){if(S.t>S.max)end(true,'max');else next(S)}}
  catch(e){err('frame',e);end(true,'error');return}
  if(ST.on)ST.raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame)}

 function camStep(dt){const S=ST.on;if(!S)return false;const sh=S.sc.shots[S.i];if(!sh)return true;try{Input.held.clear()}catch(e){}
  if(S.cutT!=null)return true;const g=camGoal(S,sh,dt),C=Camera,k=1-Math.exp(-g.k*dt),kz=1-Math.exp(-1.25*dt);
  S.cam.x+=(g.x-S.cam.x)*k;S.cam.y+=(g.y-S.cam.y)*k;S.cam.z=Math.exp(Math.log(S.cam.z)+(Math.log(cl(g.z,.5,6))-Math.log(S.cam.z))*kz);
  const sx=S.shake?(Math.random()-.5)*S.shake*2/S.cam.z:0,sy=S.shake?(Math.random()-.5)*S.shake*2/S.cam.z:0;C.x=S.cam.x+sx;C.y=S.cam.y+sy;C.zoom=S.cam.z;return true}

 function qa(){const o={why:[],scenes:0,shots:0,actors:0,offRoad:0,chapters:{},events:0,played:0,ms:0},t0=performance.now();const chk=(ok,w)=>{if(!ok)o.why.push(w);return ok};
  const keep={cam:{x:Camera.x,y:Camera.y,z:Camera.zoom},q:ST.q.slice(),on:ST.on,base:ST.base,auto:ST.auto,log:ST.log.length,fl:cloneJson(flags()||{}),pres:ST.presence.slice(),played:ST.played,skipped:ST.skipped,ended:ST.ended};

   for(const c of SDD.chapters){const n=Object.values(D.scenes).filter(s=>s.ch===c.id).length;o.chapters[c.id]=n;chk(n>=1,'ch:'+c.id)}
    
   for(const [ev,ids] of Object.entries(D.on)){const m=/^(start|done|step|chapter):(.+)$/.exec(ev);if(!chk(!!m,'ev:'+ev))continue;if(m[1]==='chapter')chk(SDD.chapters.some(c=>c.id===m[2]),'ev-ch:'+ev);
    else{const mi=m[2].split('/')[0];chk(MIS.has(mi),'ev-mi:'+ev);if(m[1]==='step'){const i=+m[2].split('/')[1],M0=SDD.chapters.flatMap(c=>c.missions||[]).find(x=>x.id===mi);chk(M0&&M0.steps[i],'ev-step:'+ev)}}for(const id of ids)chk(!!D.scenes[id],'ev-scene:'+id)}
    
   for(const id of list()){const s=scene(id);o.scenes++;let len=0;for(const sh of s.shots){o.shots++;len+=sh.d||0;
     const pp=[sh.cam&&sh.cam.poi,sh.cam&&sh.cam.from&&sh.cam.from.poi,...(sh.actors||[]).map(a=>a.at)].filter(Boolean);for(const p of pp)if(!poiXY(p))(BUILT.has(s.ch)?o.why:(o.warn=o.warn||[])).push('poi:'+id+':'+p);
     if(sh.say){chk(!sh.say[0]||!!SDD.chars[sh.say[0]],'who:'+id);chk(/[֐-׿]/.test(lineText(sh.say)),'text:'+id);if(sh.say[2])chk(!!SDD.lines[sh.say[2]],'line:'+id)}
     if(sh.title)chk(/[֐-׿]/.test(sh.title.join('')),'title:'+id);
     for(const a of sh.actors||[]){o.actors++;chk(!!D.looks[a.who],'look:'+a.who);if(a.at&&poiXY(a.at)){const sp=spotNear(poiXY(a.at),null);if(sp&&!onRoad(sp.from.x,sp.from.y)&&!onRoad(sp.to.x,sp.to.y))o.offRoad++;else(BUILT.has(s.ch)?o.why:(o.warn=o.warn||[])).push('road:'+id+':'+a.at)}}}

   chk(scenesFor('chapter:4').join()==='end:4,5-soon','ev-end4');chk(scenesFor('chapter:O').length===0,'ev-endO');o.events=7;
    
   ST.auto=false;ST.q=[];if(ST.on)end(true,'qa');ST.qaRun=true;const t1=performance.now();

    while(ST.on===S&&n++<2000){S.last-=step*1000;frame();if(ST.raf){cancelAnimationFrame(ST.raf);ST.raf=0}camStep(step)}
    chk(!ST.on,'stuck:'+id);if(ST.on)end(true,'qa');o.played++}
   o.playMs=Math.round(performance.now()-t1);
   chk(!document.body.classList.contains('tcCutOn'),'body');chk(Input.held.size===0,'input');
   chk(Math.abs(Camera.zoom-keep.cam.z)<1e-6,'zoom');

  catch(e){o.why.push('error');o.error=String(e&&e.stack||e).slice(0,300)}

   Camera.x=keep.cam.x;Camera.y=keep.cam.y;Camera.zoom=keep.cam.z;ST.q=keep.q;ST.base=keep.base;ST.auto=keep.auto;ST.log.length=Math.min(ST.log.length,keep.log);ST.played=keep.played;ST.skipped=keep.skipped;ST.ended=keep.ended;ST.presence=keep.pres;

 function holdZones(){if(T.replay)return;let F,S;try{F=fl();S=S9STORY}catch(e){return}const H=Array.isArray(F['tut:held'])?F['tut:held']:(F['tut:held']=[]);

 function releaseZones(force){let F,m;try{F=fl();m=g77St().main}catch(e){return 0}if(!Array.isArray(F['tut:held']))return 0;let mo='open';try{mo=TCSTORY.mode()}catch(e){}

  if(was&&!T.qa){try{toast(how==='skip'?'ההדרכה דולגה · אפשר להפעיל אותה שוב: טלפון ← הגדרות':'ההדרכה הושלמה')}catch(e){}save()}render()}

 function hold(isNew){let st;try{st=fl()['tut:st']}catch(e){return false}if(st==='done'||st==='skip')return false;

  if(!fresh(isNew))return false;start();return true}
 function step(){T.ticks++;if(!T.on){if(T.fin&&Date.now()>=T.finT){T.fin=null}try{if(Array.isArray(fl()['tut:held']))releaseZones()}catch(e){}render();return}

 function qa(){const P=PlayerProfile,o={why:[],steps:[]},t0=performance.now();const chk=(ok,w)=>{if(!ok)o.why.push(w);return ok};
  const keep={st:cloneJson(g77St()),mode:P.mode,car:{x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y,h:PlayerCar.heading,s:PlayerCar.speed},ped:{x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y},nav:State.navigation,phone:!!State.phoneOpen,app:State.phoneApp,iid:State.interactionId,map:!!State.mapOpen,
   T:{on:T.on,i:T.i,cx:T.cx,fin:T.fin,finT:T.finT,replay:T.replay,lastF:T.lastF,key:T.key,relT:T.relT,released:T.released},taxi:State.taxiRide,cam:[Camera.x,Camera.y]};
  T.qa={};try{
   if(State.phoneOpen)setPhoneOpen(false);if(State.mapOpen)setMapOpen(false);State.taxiRide=null;
   const F=fl();for(const k of Object.keys(F))if(/^tut:/.test(k))delete F[k];
    
   T.on=false;chk(hold(true)===true&&T.on&&F['tut:st']==='run','gate-new');
   const ids=Object.keys(S9STORY.ST),heldIds=Array.isArray(F['tut:held'])?F['tut:held'].slice():[];o.held=heldIds.length;chk(ids.every(id=>F['s9:'+id+':asked']||S9STORY.step(id)>0||S9STORY.done(id)),'quiet');
   const run=(fn,n)=>{for(let j=0;j<(n||1);j++){fn&&fn(j);const i0=T.i;const s=STEPS[T.i];if(s&&s.ok(T.cx))enter(T.i+1);if(T.i!==i0)return true}return false};
   const H=playerHomeServicePoint();
    
   P.mode='vehicle';PlayerCar.x=H.x+260;PlayerCar.y=H.y+200;o.steps.push(run(j=>{PlayerCar.x-=40},Math.ceil(m2px(26)/40)+2)?'drive':'drive✗');
    
   P.mode='walk';PlayerPed.x=H.x+150;PlayerPed.y=H.y+60;o.steps.push(run()?'exit':'exit✗');
    
   o.steps.push(run(j=>{PlayerPed.x=H.x+150-(150/8)*(j+1);PlayerPed.y=H.y+60-(60/8)*(j+1)},10)?'walk':'walk✗');
   chk(nearDoor(),'door-near');
    
   T.lastF=null;let r=false;const hail=hailNearestTaxi;hailNearestTaxi=()=>false;try{r=interactNearbyPoi()}catch(e){o.fErr=String(e).slice(0,120)}finally{hailNearestTaxi=hail}o.fOpened=State.phoneOpen?State.interactionId:null;chk(!!r&&o.fOpened==='PLAYER-HOME','F-home:'+o.fOpened);
   chk(!run(),'door-early');setPhoneOpen(false);o.steps.push(run()?'door':'door✗');
    
   setPhoneOpen(true);chk(!run(),'phone-early');setPhoneOpen(false);o.steps.push(run()?'phone':'phone✗');
   setMapOpen(true);chk(!run(),'map-early');setMapOpen(false);o.steps.push(run()?'map':'map✗');
    
   P.mode='vehicle';o.steps.push(run()?'enter':'enter✗');
   chk(!T.on&&F['tut:st']==='done','done:'+F['tut:st']);chk(hold(true)===false,'gate-open');
   chk(Array.isArray(F['tut:held']),'held-till-story');releaseZones(true);chk(!('tut:held' in F)&&heldIds.every(id=>!F['s9:'+id+':asked']||((g77St().main||{}).asked||{})[id]),'released');o.free=ids.filter(id=>!F['s9:'+id+':asked']).length;
    
   delete F['tut:st'];hold(true);chk(T.on,'restart');chk(skip()&&F['tut:st']==='skip'&&hold(true)===false,'skip');
   chk(o.steps.every(x=>!/✗/.test(x))&&o.steps.length===STEPS.length,'steps');
    
   chk((navPoiRegistry()['PLAYER-HOME']||{}).label===HOME_NAME,'name-nav');chk(MAP_MAJOR_PINS.some(p=>p[0]==='PLAYER-HOME'&&p[2]===HOME_NAME),'name-pin');
   P.mode='walk';PlayerPed.x=H.x;PlayerPed.y=H.y;const nh=nearbyPlayerHome(92);chk(nh&&nh.label===HOME_NAME,'name-F');
    
   chk(!/G77_OT\.on\b/.test(String(g77NmSkip)),'skip-flag');
   chk(!T.err,'err')}
  catch(e){o.why.push('error');o.error=String(e&&e.stack||e).slice(0,400)}
  finally{T.qa=null;try{if(State.phoneOpen)setPhoneOpen(false);if(State.mapOpen)setMapOpen(false)}catch(e){}g77StRestore(keep.st);P.mode=keep.mode;PlayerCar.x=keep.car.x;PlayerCar.y=keep.car.y;PlayerCar.heading=keep.car.h;PlayerCar.speed=keep.car.s;PlayerPed.x=keep.ped.x;PlayerPed.y=keep.ped.y;
   State.navigation=keep.nav;State.taxiRide=keep.taxi;Camera.x=keep.cam[0];Camera.y=keep.cam[1];Object.assign(T,keep.T);try{if(keep.map)setMapOpen(true);if(keep.phone){setPhoneOpen(true);State.phoneApp=keep.app;State.interactionId=keep.iid}}catch(e){}}
  o.ms=Math.round(performance.now()-t0);o.ok=!o.why.length;return o}
 {const base=roadAudit;roadAudit=function(){const r=base.apply(this,arguments);try{const q=qa(),L=r.checks||r.results;if(Array.isArray(L))L.push({name:'newgame_tutorial',id:'newgame_tutorial',ok:q.ok,pass:q.ok,detail:q.ok?'משחק חדש מחכה להדרכה · '+q.steps.length+' צעדים: '+q.steps.join(' ← ')+' · F ליד הדלת פותח את '+q.fOpened+' · '+q.held+' סיפורי אזורים בהמתנה ומשתחררים · דילוג · "'+HOME_NAME+'" · '+q.ms+' מ"ש':'נכשל: '+q.why.join(' | ')+(q.error?' · '+q.error:'')});if(!q.ok){r.ok=false;(r.issues||(r.issues=[])).push('newgame_tutorial')}}catch(e){err('audit',e)}return r}}
 const api={STEPS,T,start,skip,hold,qa,step,finish,state:()=>{let st=null;try{st=fl()['tut:st']||null}catch(e){}return{on:T.on,i:T.i,id:(STEPS[T.i]||{}).id||null,st,replay:T.replay,fin:T.fin,err:T.err,errs:T.errs,ticks:T.ticks,lastF:T.lastF&&T.lastF.id}},

var TCLOCK=(function(){

 const on=()=>{try{return ST.mode()==='story'}catch(e){return false}};
 const zoneAt=(x,y)=>{try{return currentChunkId(x,y)}catch(e){return null}};

 function sig(){const t=performance.now();if(t-E.sigT<500&&E.sig)return E.sig;E.sigT=t;let s='';if(on())for(const q of MAP_CHUNKS)if(zl(q.id))s+=q.id;if(s!==E.sig){E.sig=s;E.edge.clear();E.openNodes=null;E.bnd=null;E.reach=null;E.comp=null}return E.sig}

 function zonesNear(x,y,t){const z0=zoneAt(x,y),out=[z0];for(const [dx,dy] of [[t,0],[-t,0],[0,t],[0,-t]]){const z=zoneAt(x+dx,y+dy);if(z&&z!==z0)return null}return out}    
 function areaZones(id,S){let r=AZ.get(id);if(r)return r;const zs=new Set();try{const pts=[];if(S.start)pts.push(S.start);for(const s of S.steps||[]){if(s.poi)pts.push(s.poi);if(Array.isArray(s.drive)){const q=zonesNear(s.drive[0],s.drive[1],40);if(q)for(const z of q)if(z)zs.add(z)}}
   for(const id2 of pts){const p=poiOf(id2);if(!p)continue;const q=zonesNear(p.x,p.y,40);if(q)for(const z of q)if(z)zs.add(z)}}catch(e){err('areaZones',e)}r=[...zs];AZ.set(id,r);return r}

 function storyOpens(){try{const D=ST.D,CHO=['S','O',...D.chapters.map(c=>c.id).filter(x=>x!=='O')],add=(mid,ch,z)=>{const c=LK.zones[z];if(!c||c==='S'||CHO.indexOf(c)<=CHO.indexOf(ch))return;LK.opens=LK.opens||{};const L=LK.opens[mid]||(LK.opens[mid]=[]);if(!L.includes(z)){L.push(z);(E.opensAdded[mid]=E.opensAdded[mid]||[]).push(z)}};

    if(s.job){const d=MISSION_DEFS[s.job];if(d)for(const pid of [d.start,d.pickup,d.dropoff,...(d.stops||[])]){const p=poiOf(pid);if(p){const q=zonesNear(p.x,p.y,40);if(q)for(const z of q)add(mi.id,c.id,z)}}}
    if(s.area){const S=S9STORY.ST[s.area];if(S)for(const z of [S.zone,...areaZones(s.area,S)])add(mi.id,c.id,z)}}}catch(e){err('opens',e)}}
  
 function carrier(){if(State.taxiRide&&State.taxiRide.phase==='riding')return null;if(State.ferryRide||State.fyCar)return null;
  try{if(typeof FISH3!=='undefined'&&FISH3.S&&FISH3.S.aboard){const b=FISH3.boatById(FISH3.S.aboard);if(b)return b.mode==='player'?{k:'boat',o:b}:null}}catch(e){}
  return PlayerProfile.mode==='vehicle'?{k:'vehicle',o:PlayerCar}:{k:'walk',o:PlayerPed}}
 function bodyPts(k,o){if(k==='vehicle'){try{const b=vehicleCollisionShape(PlayerCar.asset,PlayerCar.ref||'Sedan',o.x,o.y,o.heading);return[[o.x,o.y],[b.ax,b.ay],[b.bx,b.by]]}catch(e){return[[o.x,o.y]]}}
  if(k==='boat'){const L=(o.L||30)*.5,c=Math.cos(o.h),s=Math.sin(o.h);return[[o.x,o.y],[o.x+c*L,o.y+s*L],[o.x-c*L,o.y-s*L]]}return[[o.x,o.y]]}
 const head=(k,o)=>k==='boat'?o.h:o.heading;
 function firstLock(k,o){for(const p of bodyPts(k,o)){const z=zoneAt(p[0],p[1]),L=zl(z);if(L)return{z,L}}return null}
 function bump(z,L){const t=performance.now();if(t-E.cardAt<5500&&E.cardZ===z)return;E.cardAt=t;E.cardZ=z;if(E.qa)return;try{ST.lockCard(z,L)}catch(e){try{toast(lockMsg(L,'אזור נעול ('+z+')'))}catch(x){}}}
 function revert(k,o,p){o.x=p.x;o.y=p.y;if(k==='boat'){o.h=p.h;o.u=0;o.r=0;o.vl=0;try{FISH3.placePed(o)}catch(e){}}else{o.heading=p.h;if(k==='vehicle'){o.speed=0;o.steer=0}else{o.moving=false;o.pose='idle'}}}
  
 function openNodes(){sig();if(E.openNodes)return E.openNodes;const out=[];for(const n of NODES){if(!lockedAt(n.x,n.y)&&inActiveRegion(n.x,n.y))out.push(n)}E.openNodes=out;return out}

function tcPorHash(s){let h=2166136261;s=String(s);for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++){h^=s.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}return h>>>0}
function tcPorHex(c){c=String(c||'#4f7be0').trim();if(/^#[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(c))c='#'+c[1]+c[1]+c[2]+c[2]+c[3]+c[3];return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c)?c.toLowerCase():'#4f7be0'}
 
function tcPorShade(c,k){c=tcPorHex(c);const n=parseInt(c.slice(1),16),f=v=>Math.round(k>=0?v+(255-v)*k:v*(1+k));const r=f(n>>16&255),g=f(n>>8&255),b=f(n&255);return '#'+((1<<24)|(r<<16)|(g<<8)|b).toString(16).slice(1)}
function tcPorEsc(t){return String(t==null?'':t).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[c])}
 
 
function tcPorRole(id){try{if(typeof S9STORY==='undefined')return '';const ST=S9STORY.ST,n=Object.keys(ST).length;if(TCPOR.roleN!==n){TCPOR.roleN=n;TCPOR.roleOf={};for(const S of Object.values(ST))for(const k of Object.keys(S.chars||{}))if(!TCPOR.roleOf[k])TCPOR.roleOf[k]=(S.name||'')+' '+(S.badge||'')}return TCPOR.roleOf[id]||''}catch(e){return ''}}
function tcPorLook(id,C){C=C||{};id=String(id==null?'':id);const name=String(C.name||'')+' '+tcPorRole(id),R=tcPorHash(id+'|'+name),rr=k=>((R>>>(k*3))&1023)/1023,L=TCPOR.LOOK[id]||{};
 if(L.thing||TCPOR.THING[id]||C.place)return{thing:L.thing||TCPOR.THING[id]||C.icon||'city',color:tcPorHex(C.color)};
 let age=L.age||(/סבא|זקן|הוותיק|ותיק/.test(name)?'elder':/נער|הילד|הבן של|הקטן/.test(name)?'boy':rr(0)<.17?'boy':rr(0)>.86?'elder':'man');
 const hairs=age==='elder'?['bald','short','short','side']:age==='boy'?['curly','side','wavy','short']:['short','side','curly','buzz','wavy','bald'];
 const beards=age==='boy'?['none']:age==='elder'?['full','full','short','mustache','none']:['none','none','stubble','short','full','mustache','goatee'];
 let hat=L.hat,hatCol=L.hatCol;if(!hat){for(const [re,h,col] of TCPOR.ROLE)if(re.test(name)){hat=h;hatCol=col;break}}
 if(!hat)hat=rr(5)<.45?'kippah':'none';if(!hatCol)hatCol=hat==='kippah'?TCPOR.KIP[Math.floor(rr(6)*TCPOR.KIP.length)%TCPOR.KIP.length]:hat==='hard'?'#f2c230':tcPorShade(C.color,-.15);
 const grey=age==='elder'||L.grey;
 return{age,skin:TCPOR.SKIN[(L.skin!=null?L.skin:Math.floor(rr(1)*TCPOR.SKIN.length))%TCPOR.SKIN.length],
  hair:L.hair||hairs[Math.floor(rr(2)*hairs.length)%hairs.length],
  hairCol:age==='elder'?TCPOR.GREY[Math.floor(rr(3)*4)%4]:(L.hairCol||TCPOR.HAIR[Math.floor(rr(3)*TCPOR.HAIR.length)%TCPOR.HAIR.length]),
  beardCol:age==='elder'?TCPOR.GREY[Math.floor(rr(4)*4)%4]:grey?'#8a8580':null,
  beard:L.beard||beards[Math.floor(rr(4)*beards.length)%beards.length],hat,hatCol:tcPorHex(hatCol),
  glasses:L.glasses!=null?!!L.glasses:rr(7)<(age==='elder'?.5:.16),shirt:tcPorHex(C.color),grey:!!L.grey}}

  const L=carLen(car),res=Math.max(1,Math.min(14,Math.round(Math.min(Wd*.6,Hd*.58)/L*2)/2)),R=Math.min(L*res*.62,Hd*.36),rim=U.mode==='dealer'?'#1f5fd1':U.mode==='home'?'#f2d38a':'#f2c230';
  
  c.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.35)';c.beginPath();c.arc(gx+R*.04,gy+R*.05,R*1.04,0,TAU);c.fill();c.fillStyle=rim;c.beginPath();c.arc(gx,gy,R,0,TAU);c.fill();
  let g=c.createRadialGradient(gx,gy,0,gx,gy,R);g.addColorStop(0,'#55606a');g.addColorStop(1,'#2a323a');c.fillStyle=g;c.beginPath();c.arc(gx,gy,R*.95,0,TAU);c.fill();
  for(let i=0;i<24;i++){const th=U.ang+i*PI/12,x=gx+Math.cos(th)*R*.87,y=gy+Math.sin(th)*R*.87;c.fillStyle=i===0?'rgba(255,255,255,.9)':i%6===0?'rgba(255,255,255,.5)':'rgba(255,255,255,.2)';c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,Math.max(1.5,R*(i===0?.022:.012)),0,TAU);c.fill()}
  const st=look(car),e=pic(car,st,U.ang,res);
  if(e){const q=e.q||1;c.drawImage(e.cv,gx-e.ox*q,gy-e.oy*q,e.cv.width*q,e.cv.height*q)}
  else{try{const sc=roadVehicleScaleFor(car.asset,car.ref)*res,I=TurboCity.info(car.asset,{scale:sc});c.save();c.translate(gx,gy);c.rotate(U.ang);TurboCity.draw(c,car.asset,-I.w/2,-I.h/2,{mode:'runtime',scale:sc,state:{c:st.c,r:st.r,v:st.v,L:{lights:false}},time:0});c.restore()}catch(x){err(x)}}
  if(U.fx){const u=(performance.now()-U.fx.t0)/U.fx.ms;if(u>=1)U.fx=null;else{   
    c.save();const n=40;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=i*2.399+u*3,r=R*(.35+((i*37)%60)/100)*(.6+u*.6),x=gx+Math.cos(a)*r,y=gy-R*.25+Math.sin(a)*r*.6;c.globalAlpha=(1-u)*.5;c.fillStyle=U.fx.col||'#ffffff';c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,Math.max(2,R*.035*(1+((i*13)%5)/3)),0,TAU);c.fill()}
    c.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,(1-u)*3);c.fillStyle='rgba(8,16,24,.82)';const tw=Wd*.4;c.fillRect(gx-tw/2,Hd*.015,tw,Hd*.07);c.fillStyle='#fff';c.font='800 '+Math.round(Hd*.036)+'px Segoe UI,Arial';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillText(U.fx.text,gx,Hd*.05,tw-8);c.restore()}}
  GST.n.frames++;GST.last={ms:+(performance.now()-t0).toFixed(1),res,k:((Math.round(U.ang/TAU*NDIR)%NDIR)+NDIR)%NDIR,pic:!!e,cache:U.cache.size,MB:+(U.bytes/1048576).toFixed(1)};return !!e}
  
 function tabsOf(car){if(U.mode==='dealer')return[['models','דגמים'],['c','צבע'],['r','חישוקים']];const T=[['c','צבע'],['r','חישוקים']];
  if(car&&g88RoofOk(car.asset))T.push(['v','גגון']);T.push(['up','שדרוגים']);if(U.mode==='garage')T.push(['fix','תיקון']);if(U.mode==='home'||owned().length>1)T.push(['cars','הרכבים שלי']);return T}
 function bodyHtml(car){if(!car)return '<p class="gstP">אין רכב להציג.</p>';const p=U.pre,s=car.st,lk=look(car),ro=car.loan;
  if(ro&&U.mode!=='dealer')return `<p class="gstP">הרכב הזה ${esc(car.name)} — זמני או שאול: אין בו צביעה, חישוקים ושדרוגים. את הרכב שלך מעצבים כאן כשאתה בו.</p>`;
  const now=b=>b?'<i class="gstNow">עכשיו</i>':'';
  switch(U.tab){
   case 'models':return `<div class="gstList">${Object.entries(VEHICLE_CATALOG).map(([id,c])=>{const lic=!c.license||licenseOwned(c.license);return `<button class="gstRow gstRowT${U.model===id?' on':''}" data-gst-model="${id}">${vehicleThumbHtml({vehicleId:id,ref:c.ref,colorIndex:0,rimIndex:g88RimSet(id)[0]},'vehicleThumb gstTh')}<span><b>${esc(c.name)}</b><small>${esc(c.ref||'')} · ${money(c.price)}${lic?'':' · 🔒 '+esc(c.license)}</small></span></button>`}).join('')}</div>`;
   case 'c':if(!g88Paintable(car.asset))return `<p class="gstP">לרכב הזה צבע קבוע — אין צביעה.</p>`;
    return `<div class="gstSw">${VEHICLE_COLORS.map(([nm,i])=>`<button class="gstDot${lk.c===i?' on':''}" data-gst-c="${i}" style="--c:${palHex(i)}" title="${esc(nm)}"><i></i><span>${esc(nm)}</span>${now(s.c===i)}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="gstP">לחיצה על צבע — הרכב באמצע מתחלף מיד; החיוב רק ב"קנה". ${U.mode==='dealer'?'בסוכנות — הצבע מהמפעל, בלי תוספת.':'צביעה: '+money(fee('paint'))+'.'}</p>`;
   case 'r':{const set=g88RimSet(car.asset);return `<div class="gstSw">${VEHICLE_RIMS.filter(x=>set.includes(x[1])).map(([nm,i])=>`<button class="gstRim${lk.r===i?' on':''}" data-gst-r="${i}"><canvas data-rim-sw="${i}"></canvas><span>${esc(nm)}</span>${now(s.r===i)}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="gstP">${U.mode==='dealer'?'בסוכנות — החישוקים מהמפעל, בלי תוספת.':'החלפת חישוקים: '+money(fee('rims'))+'.'} את החישוקים רואים מקרוב כאן; בעיר — מהזום הקרוב.</p>`}
   case 'v':return `<div class="gstSw">${[[0,'בלי גגון'],[1,'גגון']].map(([on,nm])=>`<button class="gstRow${lk.v===on?' on':''}" data-gst-v="${on}"><b>${nm}</b>${now(s.v===on)}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="gstP">גגון: ${money(fee('roof'))} · הסרה — בלי תשלום. את הגגון רואים גם בעולם.</p>`;
   case 'up':return UPK.map(([k,nm,what])=>{const a=car.up[k]||0,b=p.up&&p.up[k]!=null?p.up[k]:a,next=b<5?UPGRADE_PRICES[k][b]:0;
     return `<div class="gstUp"><div class="gstUpH"><b>${nm}</b><small>${what} · רמה ${a}/5${b>a?' → '+b:''}</small></div><div class="gstBar"><i class="have" style="width:${a*20}%"></i><i class="add" style="inset-inline-start:${a*20}%;width:${(b-a)*20}%"></i></div>
      <div class="gstUpB"><button data-gst-up="${k}:-1" ${b<=a?'disabled':''}>−</button><button data-gst-up="${k}:1" ${b>=5?'disabled':''}>${b>=5?'מקסימום':'+ '+money(next)}</button></div></div>`}).join('')+`<p class="gstP">ירוק — מה שיש; כתום — מה שבסל.</p>`;
   case 'fix':{const d=Math.round(Math.max(0,car.own?.damage??0)),f=Math.round(car.own?.fuel??100);return `<div class="gstUp"><div class="gstUpH"><b>נזק ${d}%</b><small>דלק ${f}% · תיקון — 6 לכל אחוז</small></div><div class="gstBar bad"><i class="have" style="width:${d}%"></i></div>
     <div class="gstUpB"><button data-gst-fix="1" class="${p.fix?'on':''}" ${d<1?'disabled':''}>${d<1?'הרכב תקין':p.fix?'✓ התיקון בסל':'הוסף תיקון לסל · '+money(Math.ceil(d*6))}</button></div></div><p class="gstP">התיקון — בתא 1 (תיקון ושדרוגים). צריך לעצור את הרכב במוסך.</p>`}
   case 'cars':return `<div class="gstList">${owned().map(v=>{const on=car.own&&car.own.ownedId===v.ownedId,act=ensureGarageSchema().activeVehicleId===v.ownedId;return `<button class="gstRow gstRowT${on?' on':''}" data-gst-car="${v.ownedId}">${vehicleThumbHtml(v,'vehicleThumb gstTh')}<span><b>${esc(VEHICLE_CATALOG[v.vehicleId]?.name||v.vehicleId)}</b><small>${esc(v.color||colName(v.colorIndex||0))} · ${esc(rimName(v.rimIndex||0))}${act?' · הרכב הפעיל':''}</small></span></button>`}).join('')}</div><p class="gstP">${U.mode==='home'?'בבית רואים את כל הרכבים שלך. החלפת הרכב הפעיל, הצבע, החישוקים והשדרוגים — במוסך העירוני D3.':'החלפת הרכב הפעיל — בכרטיס "רכבים בבעלות" למטה.'}</p>`}
  return ''}
 function basketHtml(car){const B=basket(car),m=PlayerProfile.money,nav=(()=>{try{return !!navPoiRegistry()['D3-GARAGE']}catch(e){return false}})();
  if(!B.L.length)return `<div class="gstBk empty"><span>${U.mode==='dealer'?'בוחרים דגם, צבע וחישוקים — ואז "קנה".':'בוחרים שינוי — רואים אותו על הרכב — ואז "קנה".'}</span></div>`;
  const lines=B.L.map(q=>`<span>${esc(q[1])}${q[2]?' · '+money(q[2]):''}</span>`).join('');
  if(U.mode==='home')return `<div class="gstBk"><div class="gstBkL"><b>תצוגה בלבד</b>${lines}<small>במוסך העירוני: ${money(B.total)} · הקנייה — במוסך D3.</small></div><div class="gstBkB">${nav?'<button class="gstBuy" data-nav-target="D3-GARAGE">נווט למוסך</button>':''}<button class="gstCancel" data-gst-cancel="1">ביטול</button></div></div>`;
  const can=m>=B.total;return `<div class="gstBk"><div class="gstBkL"><b>בסל</b>${lines}<small>סה״כ ${money(B.total)} · ביתרה ${money(m)}${can?'':' · חסרים '+money(B.total-m)}</small></div><div class="gstBkB"><button class="gstBuy" data-gst-buy="1" ${can?'':'disabled'}>קנה</button><button class="gstCancel" data-gst-cancel="1">ביטול</button></div></div>`}
 function shell(){return `<div class="gstStage"><div class="gstCv"><canvas id="gstMain"></canvas><span class="gstHint">גוררים כדי לסובב</span><span class="gstPre" id="gstPre">תצוגה מקדימה</span><span class="gstName" id="gstName"></span></div>
  <div class="gstCtl"><button data-gst-rot="-1" title="45° שמאלה" aria-label="45 מעלות שמאלה">⟲ 45°</button><button data-gst-auto="1" id="gstAuto"></button><button data-gst-rot="1" title="45° ימינה" aria-label="45 מעלות ימינה">45° ⟳</button></div><div id="gstBasket"></div></div>
  <div class="gstPanel"><div class="gstTabs" id="gstTabs"></div><div id="gstBody"></div></div>`}
 function refresh(){const root=$('gstRoot');if(!root)return;const car=curCar(),T=tabsOf(car);if(!T.some(t=>t[0]===U.tab))U.tab=T[0][0];
  const set=(id,h)=>{const e=$(id);if(e&&e.__h!==h){e.__h=h;e.innerHTML=h;return true}return false};
  set('gstTabs',T.map(t=>`<button class="gstTab${U.tab===t[0]?' on':''}" data-gst-tab="${t[0]}">${t[1]}</button>`).join(''));
  if(set('gstBody',bodyHtml(car))){try{g88DrawRims(root)}catch(e){err(e)}if(root.querySelector('canvas[data-vehicle-thumb]'))try{renderPhoneVehicleThumbs()}catch(e){err(e)}}
  set('gstBasket',basketHtml(car));
  const a=$('gstAuto');if(a){a.textContent=U.auto?'⏸ עצירה':'▶ סיבוב';a.classList.toggle('on',U.auto)}
  const pr=$('gstPre');if(pr)pr.style.display=dirty()?'':'none';
  const nm=$('gstName');if(nm&&car){const l=look(car);nm.textContent=car.name+' · '+colName(l.c)+' · '+rimName(l.r)+(l.v?' · גגון':'')}
  U.dirty=1;kick()}
  
 function kick(){if(!U.raf)U.raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop)}
 function loop(now){U.raf=0;if(!State.phoneOpen)return;const cv=$('gstMain');if(!cv)return;const dt=Math.min(.1,(now-(U.last||now))/1000);U.last=now;
  try{if(U.auto&&!U.drag){U.ang+=dt*.42;U.dirty=1}if(U.fx)U.dirty=1;if(U.dirty&&now-U.drawT>=30){U.drawT=now;U.dirty=0;stage(cv)}}catch(e){err(e)}
  if(U.auto||U.drag||U.dirty||U.fx)U.raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop)}
 function bindDrag(cv){if(!cv||cv.__dr)return;cv.__dr=1;cv.style.touchAction='none';
  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{U.drag={x:e.clientX,a:U.ang,id:e.pointerId};try{cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}kick()});
  cv.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!U.drag||U.drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;U.ang=U.drag.a-(e.clientX-U.drag.x)*.012;U.dirty=1;kick()});
  const up=e=>{if(U.drag&&U.drag.id===e.pointerId){U.drag=null;kick()}};cv.addEventListener('pointerup',up);cv.addEventListener('pointercancel',up)}
 function turn(sgn){const a0=U.ang,a1=(Math.round(a0/(PI/4))+sgn)*(PI/4),t0=performance.now();U.auto=false;
  const f=now=>{const u=Math.min(1,(now-t0)/260),e=u*u*(3-2*u);U.ang=a0+(a1-a0)*e;U.dirty=1;kick();if(u<1&&State.phoneOpen)requestAnimationFrame(f)};requestAnimationFrame(f);refresh()}
  
 function legacyOut(body){let n=0;const kill=sel=>body.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(b=>{const s=b.closest('section.pc');if(s&&!s.closest('#gstRoot')){s.remove();n++}});
  if(U.mode==='garage')kill('[data-garage-color],[data-garage-rim],[data-garage-roof],[data-upgrade-buy]');if(U.mode==='dealer')kill('[data-dealer-buy]');GST.n.legacyOut+=n}
 function after(){const m=modeNow(),body=$('phoneBody');if(!m||!body){if(U.mode&&!m)reset();return false}
  if(m!==U.mode){U.pre={};U.tab=null;U.auto=true;U.sel=null;U.mode=m}
  if(U.applying)return true;
  legacyOut(body);let root=$('gstRoot');if(!root||root.parentNode!==body){root=document.createElement('section');root.id='gstRoot';root.className='gst';root.innerHTML=shell();body.prepend(root);bindDrag($('gstMain'));GST.n.mount++}
  refresh();return true}
 function reset(){U.pre={};U.mode=null;U.sel=null;U.tab=null;U.auto=true;U.drag=null;U.fx=null;U.cache.clear();U.bytes=0;U.bg.clear()}   
  
 function buy(){const car=curCar();if(!car)return;const B=basket(car),newCol=palHex(look(car).c);if(!B.L.length)return;if(PlayerProfile.money<B.total)return toast('אין מספיק כסף · חסרים '+money(B.total-PlayerProfile.money));
  if(U.mode==='home')return toast('הקנייה — במוסך העירוני D3');
  const msgs=[],T0=toast;let ok=0;U.applying=true;toast=function(m){msgs.push(String(m))};
  try{if(U.mode==='dealer'){const n0=owned().length;buyDealerVehicle(car.asset);const g=ensureGarageSchema();if(g.vehicles.length>n0){ok++;const v=g.vehicles[g.vehicles.length-1],p=U.pre;
     if(p.c!=null&&g88Paintable(v.vehicleId)){v.colorIndex=p.c;v.color=colName(p.c)}if(p.r!=null&&g88RimSet(v.vehicleId).includes(p.r))v.rimIndex=p.r;try{SaveManager.save('vehicle-buy-look',{backup:false,silent:true})}catch(e){}}}
   else{const svc=nearbyD3Service(185);if(!svc||svc.id!=='D3-GARAGE')msgs.push('צריך להיות ליד המוסך D3');else if(!car.own||borrowed())msgs.push('אי אפשר לעצב רכב זמני/שאול');
    else{const id=car.own.ownedId,p=U.pre;for(const q of B.L){const k=q[0],m0=PlayerProfile.money;
      if(k==='c')paintOwnedVehicle(id,p.c);else if(k==='r')setOwnedVehicleRim(id,p.r);else if(k==='v')setOwnedVehicleRoof(id,p.v);
      else if(k==='fix')repairAtGarage();
      else if(k.startsWith('u:')){const kk=k.slice(2),to=p.up[kk];for(let i=0;i<8&&(activeOwnedVehicle().upgrades[kk]||0)<to;i++){const l0=activeOwnedVehicle().upgrades[kk]||0;buyVehicleUpgrade(kk);if((activeOwnedVehicle().upgrades[kk]||0)===l0)break}}
      if(PlayerProfile.money<m0||k==='v')ok++}}}}
  catch(e){err(e)}finally{toast=T0;U.applying=false}
  GST.n.buy++;const okAll=ok>=B.L.length||(U.mode==='dealer'&&ok>0);
  if(ok){U.pre={};U.fx={t0:performance.now(),ms:1700,text:U.mode==='dealer'?'הרכב שלך מוכן · '+car.name:B.L.some(q=>q[0]==='c')?'תא הצבע · הרכב נצבע':B.L.some(q=>q[0]==='fix')?'תא 1 · הרכב תוקן':'תא 1 · העבודה נגמרה',col:U.mode==='dealer'?'#ffffff':newCol};GST.n.fx++;try{if(typeof GHALL!=='undefined'&&U.mode==='garage')GHALL.fx={t0:performance.now(),kind:B.L.some(q=>q[0]==='c')?'paint':'work',col:newCol}}catch(e){}}
  toast((okAll?'✓ ':'')+(msgs.length?msgs.slice(-3).join(' · '):'נקנה')+(ok?' · סה״כ '+money(B.total):''));renderPhone()}
  
 function click(e){const T=e.target;if(!T||!T.closest||!T.closest('#gstRoot'))return false;const car=curCar(),b=T.closest('button');if(!b||b.disabled)return true;const d=b.dataset;
  if(d.gstTab){U.tab=d.gstTab;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstRot){turn(Number(d.gstRot)||1);return true}
  if(d.gstAuto){U.auto=!U.auto;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstC!=null){const i=Number(d.gstC);U.pre.c=car&&car.st.c===i?null:i;if(U.pre.c==null)delete U.pre.c;GST.n.pick++;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstR!=null){const i=Number(d.gstR);if(car&&car.st.r===i)delete U.pre.r;else U.pre.r=i;GST.n.pick++;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstV!=null){const i=Number(d.gstV);if(car&&car.st.v===i)delete U.pre.v;else U.pre.v=i;GST.n.pick++;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstUp){const [k,s]=d.gstUp.split(':'),a=car.up[k]||0,up=U.pre.up||(U.pre.up={}),b0=up[k]!=null?up[k]:a,b1=Math.max(a,Math.min(5,b0+Number(s)));if(b1===a)delete up[k];else up[k]=b1;if(!Object.keys(up).length)delete U.pre.up;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstFix){if(U.pre.fix)delete U.pre.fix;else U.pre.fix=true;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstCar){U.sel=d.gstCar;U.pre={};refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstModel){U.model=d.gstModel;U.pre={};refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstCancel){U.pre={};GST.n.cancel++;refresh();return true}
  if(d.gstBuy){buy();return true}
  return false}

  try { S = tcF9StoryList() } catch (e) { o.why.push('הרשימה: ' + (e && e.message)) }

  const keep = TCF9.el, d = document.createElement('div'); d.innerHTML = TCF9_TABS.map(([k]) => '<section data-pane="' + k + '"></section>').join('');
  try { TCF9.el = d; tcF9Events(); const p = d.querySelector('section[data-pane="events"]'); o.evButtons = p.querySelectorAll('button').length;
    const need = [['קווי האוטובוס', '[data-ln]'], ['העיר החיה', '[data-cl]'], ['חדר ההלבשה', '[data-wdr-f9]'], ['משחק באצטדיון', '[data-act="match"]'], ['כדור הסל', '[data-act="bk5"]'], ['הקולנוע והתיאטרון', '[data-show]'], ['הסיפורים', '[data-story]']];
    for (const [k, q] of need) if (!p.querySelector(q)) o.why.push('אירועים: אין "' + k + '"');
    const bad = [...p.querySelectorAll('.tcF9Bad')].map(x => x.textContent); if (bad.length) o.why.push('אירועים: ' + bad[0].slice(0, 160));
    let Z = []; try { Z = tcF9ZoneList() } catch (e) { o.why.push('אזורים: ' + (e && e.message)) } o.zones = Z.length; let nb = 0, lv = 0;
    for (const z of Z) { try { const h = tcF9ZoneBody(z.z, z.x, z.y); nb++; const L = window.__TC_LIVE && window.__TC_LIVE.zones && window.__TC_LIVE.zones[z.z];
        if (L) { lv++; if (!/הפרטים החיים \(\d+\)/.test(h)) o.why.push(z.z + ': אין רשימה של הפרטים החיים') } } catch (e) { o.why.push(z.z + ': ' + (e && e.message)) } }
    o.zoneBodies = nb; o.liveZones = lv; if (!Z.length) o.why.push('אין אזורים בלשונית')
  } catch (e) { o.why.push('אירועים: ' + (e && e.message)) } finally { TCF9.el = keep }
  o.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); o.ok = !o.why.length; return o }

var SACT=(function(){
 const E={err:null,armed:0,done:0,cancel:0,via:0,f:0,qa:0};const err=(w,e)=>{if(!E.err)E.err=w+': '+String(e&&e.message||e).slice(0,160)};
 const K=.6,TAU=6.2832,R0=46;
 const VERB={find:'לחפש ולהרים',take:'להרים',give:'למסור',pick:'לקטוף',work:'לעבוד',check:'לבדוק',photo:'לצלם',look:'לצפות',wear:'ללבוש',sort:'להעביר',hang:'לתלות',plant:'לשתול',via:'להמשיך'};
 const DUR={find:1.4,take:1.4,give:1.3,pick:3,work:3,check:2.6,photo:1,look:2.2,wear:1.6,sort:1.1,hang:2.2,plant:3};
 const SA={cur:null,carry:null,wear:null,res:[],fx:[]};
 const fl=()=>{try{return g77St().flags}catch(e){return{}}};
 const ST=()=>S9STORY.ST,step=id=>{try{return S9STORY.step(id)}catch(e){return 0}},done=id=>{try{return S9STORY.done(id)}catch(e){return false}};
 const spec=(id,n)=>{let a=SA_SPEC[id]&&SA_SPEC[id][n];if(!a){const d=SA_DRIVE[id]&&SA_DRIVE[id][n];if(Array.isArray(d))a=d}if(!a)return null;const o=a[4]||{};return{k:a[0],o:a[1],c:a[2],nm:a[3],opt:o,v:o.v||VERB[a[0]]}};
 const pnow=()=>performance.now();
 const walk=()=>PlayerProfile.mode==='walk';
 const me=()=>walk()?{x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y}:{x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y};
  
 function holdOf(){for(const [id,h] of Object.entries(SA_HOLD)){const n=step(id);if(n>=h[0]&&n<=h[1])return{id,o:h[2],c:h[3],nm:h[4],hold:1}}return null}
 function carryOk(){const c=SA.carry;if(!c)return;const n=step(c.id);if(n<1||done(c.id)&&n<1||(c.u&&n>c.u)){SA.carry=null;save()}}
 function carried(){carryOk();return SA.carry||holdOf()}
 function wearOk(){const w=SA.wear;if(w&&step(w.id)<1){SA.wear=null;save()}return SA.wear}
 function save(){try{const f=fl();if(SA.carry)f['sa:carry']=SA.carry;else delete f['sa:carry'];if(SA.wear)f['sa:wear']=SA.wear;else delete f['sa:wear']}catch(e){}}
 let loadedFrom=null;function load(){try{const s=g77St();if(s===loadedFrom)return;loadedFrom=s;const f=s.flags||{};SA.carry=f['sa:carry']?{...f['sa:carry']}:null;SA.wear=f['sa:wear']?{...f['sa:wear']}:null}catch(e){}}
  
  
 const DRV=[];function drvInstall(){for(const [id,M] of Object.entries(SA_DRIVE)){const S=ST()[id];if(!S)continue;for(const [ns,d] of Object.entries(M)){const n=+ns,s=S.steps[n-1];if(!s||s.__saDrive)continue;
   const real=s.drive;if(!Array.isArray(real))continue;s.__saDrive=real;const car=!Array.isArray(d)&&d.car;
   try{Object.defineProperty(s,'drive',{configurable:true,enumerable:true,get(){return car&&(!walk()||State.taxiRide&&State.taxiRide.phase==='riding')?real:[real[0],real[1],0]},set(v){}});DRV.push({id,n,real,car,s})}catch(e){err('drv',e)}}}}
 const drvNow=()=>DRV.filter(q=>step(q.id)===q.n);
 function drvTick(){for(const q of drvNow()){const a=me(),d=Math.hypot(a.x-q.real[0],a.y-q.real[1]);if(q.car){q.near=d<q.real[2];continue}
   const o=SA.cur,free=!o||o.phase==='go'&&o.id!==q.id&&Math.hypot(a.x-o.pt.x,a.y-o.pt.y)>200;if(free&&d<Math.max(q.real[2],46)){if(o)SA.cur=null;const c=arm(q.id,false);if(c&&c.k!=='find')c.R=Math.max(c.R,Math.min(130,q.real[2]))}}}
 function poiPt(id,n){const s=ST()[id].steps[n-1];if(s.__saDrive)return{x:s.__saDrive[0],y:s.__saDrive[1]};let p=null;try{p=navPoiRegistry()[s.poi]}catch(e){}if(!p&&s.drive)p={x:s.drive[0],y:s.drive[1]};if(!p)p=me();return{x:p.x,y:p.y}}
 function arm(id,quiet){const n=step(id),sp=n>0&&spec(id,n);if(!sp)return null;if(SA.cur&&SA.cur.id===id&&SA.cur.n===n&&SA.cur.phase!=='done'){if(!quiet)hint(SA.cur);return SA.cur}
   
  const P=poiPt(id,n),m0=me(),here=walk()&&Math.hypot(m0.x-P.x,m0.y-P.y)<120&&!ST()[id].steps[n-1].__saDrive,B=here?m0:P,pt=sp.opt.dx!=null?{x:B.x+sp.opt.dx,y:B.y+(sp.opt.dy||0)}:free(B.x,B.y,14,null),m=m0,d0=Math.hypot(m.x-pt.x,m.y-pt.y);
  const c={id,n,...sp,phase:'go',pt,poi:P,R:Math.min(130,Math.max(R0,d0<160?d0+14:R0)),q:sp.opt.q||1,cnt:0,t0:0,prog:0,dur:(sp.opt.t||DUR[sp.k]||2)*1000,born:pnow()};
  if(sp.k==='sort'){c.src=pt;c.dst=free(B.x,B.y,26,pt)}
  if(sp.k==='find'){const h=hsh(id+'#'+n),dd=34+h%26,q=hide(B.x,B.y,dd,h);c.pt=q;c.area={x:B.x,y:B.y,r:Math.max(56,Math.hypot(q.x-B.x,q.y-B.y)+26)};c.R=22;c.found=false}
  SA.cur=c;E.armed++;if(!quiet)hint(c);return c}
  
 function free(x,y,d,avoid){const D=[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1],[.7,.7],[-.7,.7],[.7,-.7],[-.7,-.7]];
   
  for(const off of[true,false])for(const dd of off?[d,d*1.8,d*2.8]:[d,d*2,d*3,d*4.5])for(const [ux,uy] of D){const q={x:x+ux*dd,y:y+uy*dd};if(avoid&&Math.hypot(q.x-avoid.x,q.y-avoid.y)<d*.8)continue;let ok=true;try{ok=!personBlockedAt(q.x,q.y,3)&&!personBlockedAt(x+ux*dd/2,y+uy*dd/2,3)}catch(e){ok=true}if(ok&&off){try{ok=!onRoad(q.x,q.y)}catch(e){}}if(ok)return q}
  return{x:x+(avoid?-6:6),y:y+4}}
  
 function hide(x,y,d,h){for(let k=0;k<16;k++){const a=(h%8)*TAU/8+k*TAU/16*(k%2?1:-1),dd=d*(k<8?1:.7),q={x:x+Math.cos(a)*dd,y:y+Math.sin(a)*dd};let ok=true;try{ok=!personBlockedAt(q.x,q.y,3)&&!personBlockedAt((x+q.x)/2,(y+q.y)/2,3)}catch(e){}if(ok){try{ok=!onRoad(q.x,q.y)}catch(e){}}if(ok)return q}return free(x,y,14,null)}
 function warm(c){const m=me(),d=Math.hypot(m.x-c.pt.x,m.y-c.pt.y);return d<30?'חם מאוד':d<55?'חם':d<90?'פושר':'קר'}
 function hint(c){try{toast(c.k==='find'?'לחפש — '+c.nm+(c.opt.hint?' · '+c.opt.hint:'')+' · F כשמוצאים':c.k==='via'?'הולכים עם '+c.nm+' — עד המקום':c.v+' — '+c.nm+' · F בטבעת')}catch(e){}}
  
 const cut=()=>{try{return typeof TCCUT!=='undefined'&&!!(TCCUT.playing?TCCUT.playing():TCCUT.on())}catch(e){return false}};    
 function F(){const c=SA.cur;if(!c||c.phase!=='go'||c.k==='via'||cut())return false;const m=me(),d=Math.hypot(m.x-c.pt.x,m.y-c.pt.y);if(c.k==='find'&&d>c.R){if(walk()&&Math.hypot(m.x-c.area.x,m.y-c.area.y)<c.area.r+30){try{toast('עוד לא כאן — '+warm(c))}catch(e){}return true}return false}if(d>c.R)return false;
  if(c.k==='find'){c.found=true}
  if(!walk()){try{toast('E — לצאת מהרכב, ואז F: '+c.v)}catch(e){}return true}
  if(c.k==='sort'&&c.opt.car&&!carNear(c)){try{toast('הרכב שלך רחוק — מביאים אותו עד 15 מ׳ מהארגזים')}catch(e){}return true}
  c.phase='work';c.t0=pnow();c.prog=0;c.ax=PlayerPed.x;c.ay=PlayerPed.y;c.hd=Math.atan2(c.pt.y-PlayerPed.y,c.pt.x-PlayerPed.x);if(c.k==='look'){c.tg=lookTgt(c);if(c.tg){c.dur=Math.max(c.dur,3800);c.hd=Math.atan2(c.tg.y-PlayerPed.y,c.tg.x-PlayerPed.x);E.lookReal=(E.lookReal||0)+1}}E.f++;return true}
  
 function lookTgt(c){const g=c.opt.tgt;if(!g)return null;try{if(typeof S15SIM==='undefined'||typeof s15PoseAt!=='function')return null;const clk=s15Clock(),tc=s15Tc(clk.abs),m=me(),KK=typeof S15_K!=='undefined'?S15_K:.6;let best=null,bd=1e9;
   if(g==='hgr'){const H=typeof s15HgrPose==='function'&&s15HgrPose(clk);return H&&H.p?{x:H.p[0],y:H.p[1]}:null}
   if(g.indexOf('ac')===0){for(const a of S15SIM.flights){const P=s15PoseAt(a,tc);if(!P)continue;if(g==='ac-air'&&!((P[3]||0)>0||P[4]))continue;const d=Math.hypot(P[0]-m.x,P[1]-m.y);if(d<bd){bd=d;best={x:P[0],y:P[1]-KK*(P[3]||0)}}}}
   else if(g.indexOf('veh:')===0){const re=new RegExp('^('+g.slice(4)+')-');for(const v of S15SIM.veh){if(!re.test(v.id))continue;const P=s15PoseAt(v,tc);if(!P)continue;const d=Math.hypot(P[0]-m.x,P[1]-m.y);if(d<bd){bd=d;best={x:P[0],y:P[1]}}}}
   return best&&bd<2600?best:null}catch(e){err('look',e);return null}}
 function carNear(c){try{return Math.hypot(PlayerCar.x-c.pt.x,PlayerCar.y-c.pt.y)<150}catch(e){return false}}
  
 const DRVN=Object.values(SA_DRIVE).reduce((a,M)=>a+Object.keys(M).length,0);
 const VIA=Object.keys(SA_SPEC).filter(id=>Object.values(SA_SPEC[id]).some(a=>a[0]==='via'));
 function tick(now){load();carryOk();wearOk();if(DRV.length<DRVN)drvInstall();try{drvTick()}catch(e){err('drvTick',e)}if(!SA.cur)for(const id of VIA){const n=step(id),sp=n>0&&spec(id,n);if(sp&&sp.k==='via'){arm(id,true);break}}const c=SA.cur;if(!c)return;
  if(step(c.id)!==c.n){SA.cur=null;return}
  if(c.k==='find'&&!c.found&&c.phase==='go'&&walk()){const m=me();if(Math.hypot(m.x-c.pt.x,m.y-c.pt.y)<26){c.found=true;SA.fx.push({k:'land',x:c.pt.x,y:c.pt.y,t0:now});try{toast('מצאת: '+c.nm+' — F להרים')}catch(e){}}}
  if(c.phase==='go'&&c.k==='via'){const m=me();if(walk()&&Math.hypot(m.x-c.poi.x,m.y-c.poi.y)<70){c.phase='done';E.via++;S9STORY.advance(c.id);SA.cur=null}return}
  if(c.phase!=='work')return;
  if(!walk()||Math.hypot(PlayerPed.x-c.ax,PlayerPed.y-c.ay)>26){c.phase='go';c.prog=0;E.cancel++;try{toast('הפעולה נעצרה — חוזרים ל'+c.nm+' ולוחצים F')}catch(e){}return}
  if(c.k==='look'&&c.tg){const g=lookTgt(c);if(g){c.tg=g;c.hd=Math.atan2(g.y-PlayerPed.y,g.x-PlayerPed.x)}}
  c.prog=Math.min(1,(now-c.t0)/c.dur);if(c.prog<1)return;
  if(c.k==='sort'){c.cnt++;SA.fx.push({k:'land',x:c.dst.x,y:c.dst.y,t0:now});if(c.cnt<c.q){c.phase='go';c.prog=0;try{toast(c.v+' — '+c.cnt+' מתוך '+c.q)}catch(e){}return}}
  finish(c,now)}
 function addRes(r){SA.res.push(r);if(SA.res.length>40)SA.res.splice(0,SA.res.length-40)}
 function finish(c,now){c.phase='done';E.done++;const T=now||pnow(),P=c.pt;
  const into=c.opt.into||c.o;
  if(c.k==='take'||c.k==='find')SA.carry={id:c.id,o:c.o,c:c.c,nm:c.nm,u:c.opt.u||0};
  else if(c.k==='pick'){SA.carry={id:c.id,o:into,c:c.c,nm:c.nm,u:0};addRes({o:c.o,c:c.c,x:P.x,y:P.y,t0:T,until:T+600e3,picked:1})}
  else if(c.k==='give'||c.k==='hang'){SA.carry=null;addRes({o:c.k==='hang'?'netrack':c.opt.res||c.o,c:c.c,x:P.x,y:P.y,t0:T,until:T+300e3,tick:1});if(c.opt.then){const t=c.opt.then;SA.carry={id:c.id,o:t[0],c:t[1],nm:t[2],u:0}}}
  else if(c.k==='plant'){SA.carry=null;addRes({o:'seedlings',c:c.c,x:P.x,y:P.y,t0:T,until:T+900e3,tick:1})}
  else if(c.k==='wear')SA.wear={id:c.id,vest:c.c};
  else if(c.k==='work'){if(c.opt.use)SA.carry=null;if(c.opt.res)addRes({o:c.opt.res,c:c.c,x:P.x,y:P.y,t0:T,until:T+600e3,tick:1});else if(c.opt.fx==='pack'||c.opt.fx==='stamp')addRes({o:c.opt.fx,c:c.c,x:P.x,y:P.y,t0:T,until:T+240e3,tick:1})}
  else if(c.k==='check')addRes({o:'checks',c:c.c,x:P.x,y:P.y,q:c.q,t0:T,until:T+240e3});
  else if(c.k==='photo')SA.fx.push({k:'flash',x:P.x,y:P.y,t0:T});
  else if(c.k==='sort')addRes({o:'pile',c:c.c,x:c.dst.x,y:c.dst.y,q:c.q,t0:T,until:T+300e3,car:!!c.opt.car,tick:1});
  save();SA.cur=null;try{S9STORY.advance(c.id)}catch(e){err('advance',e)}try{if(State.phoneOpen)renderPhone()}catch(e){}}
  
 const hx=h=>{const n=parseInt(h.slice(1),16);return[n>>16&255,n>>8&255,n&255]};
 const sh=(h,f)=>{const c=hx(h),m=f<0?0:255,k=Math.abs(f);return'rgb('+c.map(v=>Math.round(v+(m-v)*k)).join(',')+')'};
 function shadow(c,x,y,w,d){c.fillStyle='rgba(12,16,22,.22)';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x+1.2,y+d/2+.6,w*.62,Math.max(1.4,d*.32),0,0,TAU);c.fill()}
 function box3(c,x,y,w,d,h,col,z){z=z||0;const x0=x-w/2,y0=y-d/2,y1=y+d/2,zt=K*(z+h),zb=K*z;if(!z)shadow(c,x,y,w,d);
  c.fillStyle=sh(col,-.2);c.fillRect(x0,y1-zt,w,zt-zb);c.fillStyle=sh(col,.16);c.fillRect(x0,y0-zt,w,d);c.strokeStyle='rgba(16,20,26,.45)';c.lineWidth=.45;c.strokeRect(x0,y0-zt,w,d);c.strokeRect(x0,y1-zt,w,zt-zb)}
 function desk(c,x,y){box3(c,x,y,14,8,7,'#8a7a66');return 7}
 let BL='';    
 function thing(c,o,col,x,y,z,t){z=z||0;switch(o){
  case'crate':box3(c,x,y,8,6,5,col,z);c.strokeStyle='rgba(60,40,20,.5)';c.lineWidth=.45;for(const f of[.33,.66]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-4,y+3-K*(z+5*f));c.lineTo(x+4,y+3-K*(z+5*f));c.stroke()}break;
  case'box':box3(c,x,y,7,6,5,col,z);c.fillStyle='rgba(120,90,50,.55)';c.fillRect(x-.7,y-3-K*(z+5),1.4,6);break;
  case'case':box3(c,x,y,6,4,5,col,z);c.strokeStyle='#222';c.lineWidth=.7;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-1.8,y-.6-K*(z+5));c.quadraticCurveTo(x,y-3-K*(z+5),x+1.8,y-.6-K*(z+5));c.stroke();break;
  case'basket':box3(c,x,y,7,5,3.5,'#a0763f',z);c.fillStyle=col;for(let i=0;i<5;i++){c.beginPath();c.arc(x-2.6+i*1.3,y-1.1-K*(z+3.5)+(i%2)*.9,1.05,0,TAU);c.fill()}break;
  case'seed':box3(c,x,y,7,5,2,'#6b4a2a',z);c.fillStyle=col;for(let i=0;i<6;i++){c.beginPath();c.arc(x-2.4+(i%3)*2.4,y-1.3+Math.floor(i/3)*2.2-K*(z+3),1,0,TAU);c.fill()}break;
  case'bag':shadow(c,x,y,5,3);c.fillStyle=col;c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x,y-K*(z+3),2.8,3,0,0,TAU);c.fill();c.strokeStyle='rgba(16,20,26,.45)';c.lineWidth=.45;c.stroke();break;
  case'ball':shadow(c,x,y,3.6,2.2);c.fillStyle=col;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y-K*(z+1.8)-.6,1.8,0,TAU);c.fill();c.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.7)';c.beginPath();c.arc(x-.6,y-K*(z+1.8)-1.2,.6,0,TAU);c.fill();c.strokeStyle='#c0392b';c.lineWidth=.4;c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y-K*(z+1.8)-.6,1.8,.3,2.2);c.stroke();break;
  case'long':box3(c,x,y,22,4,1.2,col,z);break;
  case'suitcase':box3(c,x,y,5.5,3,7,col,z);c.strokeStyle='#222';c.lineWidth=.6;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-1.4,y-1.5-K*(z+7));c.lineTo(x-1.4,y-2.8-K*(z+7));c.lineTo(x+1.4,y-2.8-K*(z+7));c.lineTo(x+1.4,y-1.5-K*(z+7));c.stroke();break;
  case'paper':{const zt=z||desk(c,x,y);box3(c,x,y,3.6,4.6,.3,col,zt);c.strokeStyle='rgba(60,60,60,.45)';c.lineWidth=.3;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-1.2,y-1.4+i*1.1-K*(zt+.3));c.lineTo(x+1.2,y-1.4+i*1.1-K*(zt+.3));c.stroke()}break}
  case'small':{const zt=z||desk(c,x,y);box3(c,x,y,2.4,1.8,1.2,col,zt);const g=.5+.5*Math.sin((t||0)/180);c.fillStyle='rgba(255,246,200,'+(.35+.5*g)+')';c.beginPath();c.arc(x+1,y-1-K*(zt+1.6),.9,0,TAU);c.fill();break}
  case'net':shadow(c,x,y,10,6);c.strokeStyle=col;c.lineWidth=.6;for(let i=-4;i<=4;i+=2){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+i,y-3);c.lineTo(x+i*.6,y+3);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-5,y+i*.7);c.lineTo(x+5,y+i*.7);c.stroke()}break;
  case'netrack':for(const s of[-7,7]){box3(c,x+s,y,1,1,16,'#6b4a2a',z)}c.fillStyle='#6b4a2a';c.fillRect(x-7.5,y-K*16-.6,15,1.2);c.strokeStyle=col;c.lineWidth=.55;for(let i=-6;i<=6;i+=1.5){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+i,y-K*16);c.lineTo(x+i+Math.sin((t||0)/600+i)*.6,y-K*3);c.stroke()}for(let j=0;j<5;j++){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-6.5,y-K*(15-j*2.6));c.lineTo(x+6.5,y-K*(15-j*2.6));c.stroke()}break;
  case'tripod':c.strokeStyle='#3a3d42';c.lineWidth=.8;for(const [dx,dy] of[[-4,2],[4,2],[0,-3]]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y-K*14);c.lineTo(x+dx,y+dy);c.stroke()}box3(c,x,y-.5,4,2.5,2.4,'#222428',14);break;
  case'flag':box3(c,x,y,1,1,40,'#c8cdd2');{const w=Math.sin((t||0)/260);c.fillStyle=col;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+.5,y-K*40);c.quadraticCurveTo(x+6,y-K*40-1.5+w,x+12,y-K*39+w*1.4);c.lineTo(x+12,y-K*33+w*1.4);c.quadraticCurveTo(x+6,y-K*34+1.5+w,x+.5,y-K*34);c.closePath();c.fill()}break;
  case'pole':box3(c,x,y,1.2,1.2,40,'#c8cdd2');break;
  case'post':box3(c,x,y,2,2,12,'#8a6a3a');c.fillStyle=col;c.fillRect(x-1,y+1-K*10.5,2,K*2.4);c.fillStyle=sh(col,.25);c.fillRect(x-1,y-1-K*12,2,2);break;
  case'board':box3(c,x,y,1,1,13,'#5b6672');box3(c,x,y-.5,14,1.2,8,col,12);c.fillStyle='#e8eef4';if(BL){c.font='700 '+(BL.length>5?2.2:3.4)+'px Segoe UI,Arial';c.textAlign='center';c.fillText(BL,x,y+.2-K*16)}else for(let i=0;i<3;i++)c.fillRect(x-4.5,y-1.6+i*1.5-K*16,i===2?5:9,.55);break;
  case'plate':box3(c,x,y,12,1.2,7,col,3);c.fillStyle='#f4f4f0';c.fillRect(x-3,y+.6-K*7.5,6,2.2);break;
  case'buoy':break;
  case'rack':box3(c,x,y,1,1,17,'#6b6f75');c.fillStyle='#6b6f75';c.fillRect(x-5,y-K*17-.5,10,1);if(SA.wear)break;c.fillStyle=col;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-3,y-K*16.5);c.lineTo(x+3,y-K*16.5);c.lineTo(x+3.6,y-K*9);c.lineTo(x-3.6,y-K*9);c.closePath();c.fill();c.strokeStyle='rgba(200,200,200,.9)';c.lineWidth=.5;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-3.4,y-K*11.5);c.lineTo(x+3.4,y-K*11.5);c.stroke();break;
  case'dev':box3(c,x,y,9,5,13,col,z);break;
  case'plants':for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const px=x-9+i*9,py=y+(i%2)*2;shadow(c,px,py,7,4);c.fillStyle=i%2?'#3f8a3a':'#4a9a44';c.beginPath();c.arc(px,py-K*5,4.6,0,TAU);c.fill();c.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.12)';c.beginPath();c.arc(px-1.4,py-K*5-1.4,2,0,TAU);c.fill()}break;
  case'wheat':for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const px=x-8+(i%3)*8,py=y-3+Math.floor(i/3)*3;c.strokeStyle='#b8943a';c.lineWidth=.6;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(px,py);c.lineTo(px+.6,py-K*9);c.stroke();c.fillStyle=col;c.beginPath();c.ellipse(px+.7,py-K*9-1,.9,2,.2,0,TAU);c.fill()}break;
  case'marks':case'checks':break;
  default:box3(c,x,y,8,6,6,col,z)}}
 function fruit(c,x,y,col,left){c.fillStyle=col;for(let i=0;i<3;i++)for(let j=0;j<3;j++){if(i*3+j>=left)continue;c.beginPath();c.arc(x-9+i*9-1.6+j*1.6,y+(i%2)*2-K*5-1+(j%2)*1.6,1.1,0,TAU);c.fill()}}
 function ring(c,x,y,r,now,lab){const q=.5+.5*Math.sin(now/260);c.save();c.strokeStyle='rgba(84,214,150,'+(.5+.4*q)+')';c.lineWidth=2;c.setLineDash([6,4]);c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,r*(.9+.08*q),0,TAU);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);
  c.fillStyle='rgba(18,26,34,.78)';c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y-K*26,5.4,0,TAU);c.fill();c.fillStyle='#fff';c.font='800 '+(lab&&lab.length>1?4.4:7)+'px Segoe UI,Arial';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillText(lab||'F',x,y-K*26+.3);c.restore()}
 function tickMark(c,x,y,a){c.save();c.globalAlpha=a;c.fillStyle='#2f9e5f';c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,3.4,0,TAU);c.fill();c.strokeStyle='#fff';c.lineWidth=1;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-1.6,y);c.lineTo(x-.3,y+1.4);c.lineTo(x+1.8,y-1.3);c.stroke();c.restore()}
 const inView=(x,y,r)=>{try{return typeof e3InView!=='function'||e3InView(x-r,y-r,2*r,2*r,30)}catch(e){return true}};
  
 function drawWorld(now){const c=ctx;if(!(Camera.zoom>=.32))return;
  for(let i=SA.res.length-1;i>=0;i--){const r=SA.res[i];if(now>r.until){SA.res.splice(i,1);continue}if(!inView(r.x,r.y,40))continue;
   if(r.o==='green'){thing(c,'dev',r.c,r.x,r.y,0,now);lamp(c,r.x,r.y,true,now)}
   else if(r.o==='checks'){for(let k=0;k<(r.q||1);k++){const p=chkPt(r,k);thing(c,'post',r.c,p.x,p.y,0,now);tickMark(c,p.x,p.y-K*20,1)}}
   else if(r.o==='pile'){if(!r.car)for(let k=0;k<r.q;k++)thing(c,'crate',r.c,r.x+(k%2)*4,r.y,Math.floor(k/2)*5,now)}
   else if(r.o==='stamp'||r.o==='pack'){if(r.o==='pack')thing(c,'box',r.c,r.x,r.y,0,now);else{const z=desk(c,r.x,r.y);box3(c,r.x,r.y,6,7,.4,'#f1ece0',z);c.strokeStyle='#2f7d5a';c.lineWidth=.7;c.beginPath();c.arc(r.x+1,r.y-K*(z+.4),1.8,0,TAU);c.stroke()}}
   else if(r.o==='seedlings'){for(let k=0;k<5;k++){const px=r.x-8+k*4,py=r.y+(k%2)*2;c.strokeStyle='#5aa04a';c.lineWidth=.8;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(px,py);c.lineTo(px,py-K*4);c.stroke();c.fillStyle='#6cc05a';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(px-1,py-K*4,1.3,.7,-.5,0,TAU);c.ellipse(px+1,py-K*4.4,1.3,.7,.5,0,TAU);c.fill()}}
   else thing(c,r.o,r.c,r.x,r.y,0,now);
   if(r.tick){const a=Math.max(0,1-(now-r.t0)/1600);if(a>0)tickMark(c,r.x,r.y-K*24-(1-a)*8,a)}}
  for(const d of cut()?[]:drvNow()){if(SA.cur&&SA.cur.id===d.id)continue;const [x,y,r]=d.real;if(!inView(x,y,80))continue;if(d.car){if(walk())ring(c,x,y,Math.min(r,60),now,'רכב')}else ring(c,x,y,Math.min(r,40),now,'F')}
  const q=SA.cur;if(!q||!inView(q.pt.x,q.pt.y,60))return;
  if(q.k==='via'){ring(c,q.poi.x,q.poi.y,40,now,'→');return}
  const P=q.pt,w=q.phase==='work'?q.prog:0;
  if(q.k==='take'&&!(w>=.55))thing(c,q.o,q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);
  if(q.k==='find'){if(!q.found&&q.phase==='go'&&!cut()){ring(c,q.area.x,q.area.y,q.area.r,now,'?')}else if(!(w>=.55)){thing(c,q.o,q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);if(q.phase==='go'){const g=.5+.5*Math.sin(now/140);c.save();c.strokeStyle='rgba(255,236,150,'+(.4+.5*g)+')';c.lineWidth=.8;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const a=i*TAU/4+now/700;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(P.x+Math.cos(a)*4,P.y-K*4+Math.sin(a)*3);c.lineTo(P.x+Math.cos(a)*7,P.y-K*4+Math.sin(a)*5);c.stroke()}c.restore()}}}
  if(q.k==='pick'){thing(c,q.o,q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);if(q.o==='plants')fruit(c,P.x,P.y,q.c,Math.round(9*(1-w)))}
  if(q.k==='give'&&w>=.55)thing(c,q.opt.res||q.o,q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);
  if(q.k==='hang'){if(w<.55)thing(c,'net',q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);else thing(c,'netrack',q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now)}
  if(q.k==='wear')thing(c,'rack',q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);
  if(q.k==='work'||q.k==='check'){if(q.o==='dev'){thing(c,'dev',q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now);lamp(c,P.x,P.y,false,now)}else if(q.o==='post')thing(c,'post',w>.5?q.c:'#9a8a70',P.x,P.y,0,now);else if(q.o==='flag'){thing(c,'pole',q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now)}else if(q.o==='marks'){for(let k=0;k<q.q;k++){const p=chkPt({x:P.x,y:P.y,q:q.q},k);thing(c,'post','#9a8a70',p.x,p.y,0,now);if(w>(k+1)/(q.q+.2))tickMark(c,p.x,p.y-K*20,1)}}else if(q.o!=='none'&&!(carried()&&carried().o===q.o&&w===0)){BL=q.opt.lab||'';try{thing(c,q.o,q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now)}finally{BL=''}}}
  if(q.k==='photo'||q.k==='look'){if(q.o!=='sky'&&q.o!=='buoy'&&q.o!=='span')thing(c,q.o,q.c,P.x,P.y,0,now)}
  if(q.k==='sort'){const left=q.q-q.cnt-(w>0?1:0);for(let k=0;k<left;k++)thing(c,q.o,q.c,q.src.x+(k%2)*4,q.src.y,Math.floor(k/2)*5,now);const dst=q.opt.car?{x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y}:q.dst;if(!q.opt.car)for(let k=0;k<q.cnt;k++)thing(c,q.o,q.c,dst.x+(k%2)*4,dst.y,Math.floor(k/2)*5,now);
   if(w>0){const a=q.src,b=dst,f=w*w*(3-2*w),x=a.x+(b.x-a.x)*f,y=a.y+(b.y-a.y)*f;thing(c,q.o,q.c,x,y,10+Math.sin(Math.PI*f)*6,now)}}
  if(q.phase==='go'&&!cut()&&(q.k!=='find'||q.found))ring(c,P.x,P.y,Math.min(q.R,30),now,'F');
  if(q.phase==='work'){c.save();c.strokeStyle='rgba(18,26,34,.55)';c.lineWidth=3;c.beginPath();c.arc(PlayerPed.x,PlayerPed.y-K*22,6,0,TAU);c.stroke();c.strokeStyle='#54d696';c.lineWidth=2.2;c.beginPath();c.arc(PlayerPed.x,PlayerPed.y-K*22,6,-Math.PI/2,-Math.PI/2+TAU*w);c.stroke();c.restore();
   if(q.opt.fx==='sparks'||q.opt.fx==='paint'){const n=q.opt.fx==='paint'?3:6;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=(now/90+i*1.7)%TAU,r=2+((now/40+i*13)%5);c.strokeStyle=q.opt.fx==='paint'?q.c:'rgba(255,214,90,.95)';c.lineWidth=.7;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(P.x,P.y-K*8);c.lineTo(P.x+Math.cos(a)*r,P.y-K*8+Math.sin(a)*r*.6);c.stroke()}}
   if(q.opt.fx==='radio'){c.save();c.strokeStyle='rgba(84,170,255,.85)';c.lineWidth=.9;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const r=((now/420+i/3)%1)*16+3;c.globalAlpha=1-r/19;c.beginPath();c.arc(PlayerPed.x+2,PlayerPed.y-K*17,r,-1.1,.2);c.stroke()}c.restore()}
   if(q.k==='photo'){const s=16-8*w;c.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.95)';c.lineWidth=1.1;for(const [sx,sy] of[[-1,-1],[1,-1],[1,1],[-1,1]]){const cx=P.x+sx*s,cy=P.y-K*10+sy*s*.7;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx,cy-sy*4);c.lineTo(cx,cy);c.lineTo(cx-sx*4,cy);c.stroke()}}
   if(q.k==='look'){const tx=q.tg?q.tg.x:PlayerPed.x+Math.cos(q.hd||-Math.PI/2)*60,ty=q.tg?q.tg.y:PlayerPed.y+Math.sin(q.hd||-Math.PI/2)*60,r=q.tg?Math.max(26,70/Math.max(.3,Camera.zoom)):26;c.save();c.fillStyle='rgba(8,12,18,'+(.38*Math.min(1,w*3))+')';c.beginPath();c.rect(Camera.x-4000,Camera.y-4000,8000,8000);c.arc(tx,ty,r,0,TAU,true);c.fill('evenodd');c.strokeStyle='rgba(20,24,30,.8)';c.lineWidth=2.4;c.beginPath();c.arc(tx,ty,r,0,TAU);c.stroke();c.restore()}}}
 function lamp(c,x,y,ok,now){const on=ok||Math.sin(now/170)>0;c.fillStyle=ok?'#38d070':on?'#ff4a3a':'#5a2a26';c.beginPath();c.arc(x+2.6,y+2.5-K*11,1.3,0,TAU);c.fill();if(on){c.fillStyle=ok?'rgba(56,208,112,.28)':'rgba(255,74,58,.28)';c.beginPath();c.arc(x+2.6,y+2.5-K*11,3.4,0,TAU);c.fill()}}
 const chkPt=(r,k)=>{const n=r.q||1;return{x:r.x+(k-(n-1)/2)*16,y:r.y+(k%2)*5}};
  
 function p34Live(){try{return typeof P34G!=='undefined'&&P34G.live&&p34On()}catch(e){return false}}
  
 function drawTrunk(now){const h=carried();if(!h||PlayerProfile.mode!=='vehicle'||State.taxiRide||State.ferryRide)return;try{if(typeof PlayerCar==='undefined'||!inView(PlayerCar.x,PlayerCar.y,60))return;const a=PlayerCar.heading||0,x=PlayerCar.x-Math.cos(a)*15,y=PlayerCar.y-Math.sin(a)*15;
   thing(ctx,h.o==='plants'?'crate':h.o,h.c,x,y,11,now);E.trunk=(E.trunk||0)+1}catch(e){err('trunk',e)}}
 function drawHand(now){const c=ctx,h=carried(),q=SA.cur;if(!walk()){drawTrunk(now);return}let o=h&&h.o,col=h&&h.c;

 function along(v,s){const P=v.path;for(let i=1;i<P.length;i++){const a=P[i-1],b=P[i],l=Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.y-a.y);if(s<=l||i===P.length-1){const f=l>0?Math.min(1,Math.max(0,s/l)):1;return{x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*f,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*f}}s-=l}return P[P.length-1]}
 const VDUR={lift:2.6,down:1.8,gate:1.1,up:1.5,dn:1.1};
 function visTick(now){const v=VIS.v;if(!v)return false;
  if(!walk()||State.taxiRide||State.ferryRide){visEnd(false);return false}
  const t=(now-v.t0)/1000,go=ph=>{v.ph=ph;v.t0=now};

  if(v.ph==='in'||v.ph==='back'){const s=Math.min(v.L,t*v.sp),p=along(v,v.ph==='in'?s:v.L-s);PlayerPed.x=p.x;PlayerPed.y=p.y;PlayerPed.moving=true;v.hold={x:p.x,y:p.y};v.hide=false;
   if(s>=v.L){PlayerPed.moving=false;if(v.ph==='in'){go(v.kind==='roof'?'lift':'gate');v.camOn=v.kind==='roof'}else{visEnd(true);return true}}return true}
  PlayerPed.moving=false;v.hold={x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y};
  if(v.ph==='lift'){v.hide=true;v.camOn=true;if(t>=VDUR.lift){go('on');try{toast('על הגג של מגדל הכיכר · המשקפת · E — לרדת')}catch(e){}}}
  else if(v.ph==='gate'){v.hide=false;if(t>=VDUR.gate){go('up');v.camOn=true}}
  else if(v.ph==='up'){v.hide=true;if(t>=VDUR.up){go('on');let on=false;try{on=plcMatchOn()}catch(e){}try{toast(on?'⚽ ביציע הדרומי, שורה '+v.D.row+' · E — לצאת':'ביציע — המגרש ריק עכשיו · E — לצאת')}catch(e){}}}
  else if(v.ph==='on'){v.hide=true;if(v.kind==='stand'&&v.match){let on=true;try{on=plcMatchOn()}catch(e){}if(!on&&!v.endAt){v.endAt=now+2500;try{toast('השריקה — המשחק נגמר, יוצאים מהיציע')}catch(e){}}if(v.endAt&&now>=v.endAt)visOut(now)}}
  else if(v.ph==='down'){v.hide=true;if(t>=VDUR.down){go('back');v.camOn=false;v.hold=null}}

 function visOut(now){const v=VIS.v;if(!v)return false;const T=now||pnow(),f=Math.min(1,(T-v.t0)/1000/(VDUR[v.ph]||1));
